Homage to that Blessed One, the perfected one, the fully awakened Buddha.

# In the Abhidhamma Collection ^abhidhamma-0

## Dhammasaṅgaṇī ^1-0

### Outline ^1-0-0

#### Threefold Outline ^1-0a-0

1. (A) things good for the mind.
(B) things bad for the mind.
(C) things neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-0a-1

2. (A) things associated with how something feels good.
(B) things associated with how something feels bad.
(C) things associated with how something feels neither good nor bad. ^1-0a-2

3. (A) things that are results of action.
(B) things that are both results and causes of results.
(C) things that are neither results nor causes of results. ^1-0a-3

4. (A) things already grasped and that lead to more grasping.
(B) things not yet grasped but that could lead to grasping.
(C) things neither grasped nor leading to grasping. ^1-0a-4

5. (A) things that are stained and that stain the mind.
(B) things not stained but that can stain the mind.
(C) things neither stained nor staining. ^1-0a-5

6. (A) things with putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something.
(B) things with only keeping the mind on something.
(C) things without putting the mind onto something or keeping the mind on something. ^1-0a-6

7. (A) things accompanied by a feeling of joy.
(B) things accompanied by ease and comfort.
(C) things accompanied by calm balance. ^1-0a-7

8. (A) things to be removed by insight.
(B) things to be removed by developing.
(C) things to be removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-0a-8

9. (A) things that are causes of what is removed by insight.
(B) things that are causes of what is removed by developing.
(C) things that are causes of what is removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-0a-9

10. (A) things that lead to increase.
(B) things that lead to decrease.
(C) things that lead neither to increase nor to decrease. ^1-0a-10

11. (A) things for those still training.
(B) things for those who have finished training.
(C) things for neither those still training nor those who have finished training. ^1-0a-11

12. (A) limited things.
(B) powerful, elevated things.
(C) immeasurable things. ^1-0a-12

13. (A) things focused on limited objects.
(B) things focused on powerful objects.
(C) things focused on immeasurable objects. ^1-0a-13

14. (A) low things.
(B) middle things.
(C) excellent things. ^1-0a-14

15. (A) things fixed on what is wrong.
(B) things fixed on what is right.
(C) things not fixed either way. ^1-0a-15

16. (A) things with the path as their object.
(B) things that are causes of the path.
(C) things that guide the path. ^1-0a-16

17. (A) things that have arisen.
(B) things that have not arisen.
(C) things that give rise to new things. ^1-0a-17

18. (A) past things.
(B) future things.
(C) present things. ^1-0a-18

19. (A) things with a past object.
(B) things with a future object.
(C) things with a present object. ^1-0a-19

20. (A) inner things.
(B) external things.
(C) things both inner and external. ^1-0a-20

21. (A) things with an inner object.
(B) things with an external object.
(C) things with an object both inner and external. ^1-0a-21

22. (A) visible things that push back when touched.
(B) invisible things that push back when touched.
(C) invisible things that do not push back when touched.
Threefold Outline. ^1-0a-22

### Section on States of Mind ^1-1-0

#### Wholesome Mind in the Sense-Desire Level ^1-1-1-0

##### Word Analysis ^1-1-1-1-0

1. Which things are good for the mind? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense-desire level has arisen, accompanied by a happy feeling and associated with knowledge, with a visible form, sound, smell, taste, touch, or mental object as object — at that time there is contact, feeling, perceiving, intending, the mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, ease, the mind staying on one thing, the faculty of faith, the faculty of effort, the faculty of paying close attention, the faculty of calm focus, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of the mind, the faculty of happy feeling, the faculty of life, right view, right intention, right effort, right paying close attention, right calm focus, the power of faith, the power of effort, the power of paying close attention, the power of calm focus, the power of wisdom, the power of conscience, the power of concern about wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hatred, wisdom, non-coveting, non-ill-will, right view, conscience, concern about wrongdoing, calm in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, calm in the mind, lightness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, lightness in the mind, softness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, softness in the mind, fitness for use in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, fitness for use in the mind, readiness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, readiness in the mind, straightness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, straightness in the mind, paying close attention, clear awareness, stilling of the mind, seeing things clearly as they are, effort, calmness without distraction; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-1

2. What is contact at that time? Whatever contact, touching, and being touched there is at that time — that is contact at that time. ^1-2

3. What is feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-3

4. What is perceiving at that time? Whatever perceiving and recognizing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is perceiving at that time. ^1-4

5. What is intending at that time? Whatever intending and willing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is intending at that time. ^1-5

6. What is the mind at that time? Whatever mind, heart, and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the mind at that time. ^1-6

7. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? Whatever placing of the mind and right intention there is at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-7

8. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? Whatever sustained application and examination of the mind there is at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-8

9. What is a feeling of joy at that time? Whatever joy, gladness, and delight in the mind there is at that time — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-9

10. What is ease at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of mind-contact there is at that time — that is ease at that time. ^1-10

11. What is the mind staying on one thing at that time? Whatever steadiness, calm focus, and right calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the mind staying on one thing at that time. ^1-11

12. What is the faculty of faith at that time? Whatever faith and confidence there is at that time — that is the faculty of faith at that time. ^1-12

13. What is the faculty of effort at that time? Whatever starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind there is at that time — that is the faculty of effort at that time. ^1-13

14. What is the faculty of paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention and not forgetting there is at that time — that is the faculty of paying close attention at that time. ^1-14

15. What is the faculty of calm focus at that time? Whatever steadiness and calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the faculty of calm focus at that time. ^1-15

16. What is the faculty of wisdom at that time? Whatever wisdom that examines and understands clearly there is at that time — that is the faculty of wisdom at that time. ^1-16

17. What is the faculty of the mind at that time? Whatever mind and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the faculty of the mind at that time. ^1-17

18. What is the faculty of happy feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling there is at that time — that is the faculty of happy feeling at that time. ^1-18

19. What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever life-force that keeps formless things going there is at that time — that is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-19

20. What is right view at that time? Whatever wisdom that understands in the right way there is at that time — that is right view at that time. ^1-20

21. What is right intention at that time? Whatever putting the mind onto something in the right way there is at that time — that is right intention at that time. ^1-21

22. What is right effort at that time? Whatever effort in the right way there is at that time — that is right effort at that time. ^1-22

23. What is right paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention in the right way there is at that time — that is right paying close attention at that time. ^1-23

24. What is right calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind in the right way there is at that time — that is right calm focus at that time. ^1-24

25. What is the power of faith at that time? Whatever faith there is at that time — that is the power of faith at that time. ^1-25

26. What is the power of effort at that time? Whatever effort there is at that time — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-26

27. What is the power of paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention there is at that time — that is the power of paying close attention at that time. ^1-27

28. What is the power of calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the power of calm focus at that time. ^1-28

29. What is the power of wisdom at that time? Whatever wisdom there is at that time — that is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-29

30. What is the power of conscience at that time? Whatever conscience about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of conscience at that time. ^1-30

31. What is the power of concern about wrongdoing at that time? Whatever concern about wrongdoing regarding bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of concern about wrongdoing at that time. ^1-31

32. What is non-greed at that time? Whatever absence of greed and non-coveting there is at that time — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-greed at that time. ^1-32

33. What is non-hatred at that time? Whatever absence of hatred and non-ill-will there is at that time — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-hatred at that time. ^1-33

#### Wholesome Mind in the Formless Level ^1-1-3-0

##### Four Formless Meditations, Sixteenfold ^1-1-3-1-0

##### Pure Practice ^1-1-5-1-0

277. Which things are good for the mind? At a time when one develops a supra-mundane state of deep calm in meditation that leads out and leads to decrease, for abandoning wrong views, for reaching the first level, detached from sense desires…pe… one reaches and stays in the first state of deep calm in meditation, practicing with difficulty and slow understanding — at that time there is contact, feeling, perceiving, intending, the mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, ease, the mind staying on one thing, the faculty of faith, the faculty of effort, the faculty of paying close attention, the faculty of calm focus, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of the mind, the faculty of happy feeling, the faculty of life, the faculty of "I shall come to know what is not yet known," right view, right intention, right speech, right action, right livelihood, right effort, right paying close attention, right calm focus, the power of faith, the power of effort, the power of paying close attention, the power of calm focus, the power of wisdom, the power of conscience, the power of concern about wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hatred, wisdom, non-coveting, non-ill-will, right view, conscience, concern about wrongdoing, calm in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, calm in the mind, lightness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, lightness in the mind, softness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, softness in the mind, fitness for use in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, fitness for use in the mind, readiness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, readiness in the mind, straightness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits, straightness in the mind, paying close attention, clear awareness, stilling of the mind, seeing things clearly as they are, effort, calmness without distraction; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-277

278. What is contact at that time? Whatever contact, touching, and being touched there is at that time — that is contact at that time. ^1-278

279. What is feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-279

280. What is perceiving at that time? Whatever perceiving and recognizing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is perceiving at that time. ^1-280

281. What is intending at that time? Whatever intending and willing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is intending at that time. ^1-281

282. What is the mind at that time? Whatever mind, heart, and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the mind at that time. ^1-282

283. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? Whatever placing of the mind and right intention there is at that time — a path factor, included in the path — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-283

284. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? Whatever sustained application and examination of the mind there is at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-284

285. What is a feeling of joy at that time? Whatever joy, gladness, and delight in the mind there is at that time — a factor of awakening — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-285

286. What is ease at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of mind-contact there is at that time — that is ease at that time. ^1-286

287. What is the mind staying on one thing at that time? Whatever steadiness, calm focus, and right calm focus of the mind there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the mind staying on one thing at that time. ^1-287

288. What is the faculty of faith at that time? Whatever faith and confidence there is at that time — that is the faculty of faith at that time. ^1-288

289. What is the faculty of effort at that time? Whatever starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the faculty of effort at that time. ^1-289

290. What is the faculty of paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention and not forgetting there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the faculty of paying close attention at that time. ^1-290

291. What is the faculty of calm focus at that time? Whatever steadiness and calm focus of the mind there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the faculty of calm focus at that time. ^1-291

292. What is the faculty of wisdom at that time? Whatever wisdom that examines and understands clearly there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the faculty of wisdom at that time. ^1-292

293. What is the faculty of the mind at that time? Whatever mind and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the faculty of the mind at that time. ^1-293

294. What is the faculty of happy feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling there is at that time — that is the faculty of happy feeling at that time. ^1-294

295. What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever life-force that keeps formless things going there is at that time — that is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-295

296. What is the faculty of "I shall come to know what is not yet known" at that time? Whatever wisdom for realizing what has not yet been known, seen, reached, or directly experienced there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the faculty of "I shall come to know what is not yet known" at that time. ^1-296

297. What is right view at that time? Whatever wisdom that understands in the right way there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is right view at that time. ^1-297

298. What is right intention at that time? Whatever putting the mind onto something in the right way there is at that time — a path factor, included in the path — that is right intention at that time. ^1-298

299. What is right speech at that time? Whatever abstaining, turning away, and refraining from the four wrong ways of speech there is at that time — a path factor, included in the path — that is right speech at that time. ^1-299

300. What is right action at that time? Whatever abstaining, turning away, and refraining from the three wrong ways of bodily action there is at that time — a path factor, included in the path — that is right action at that time. ^1-300

301. What is right livelihood at that time? Whatever abstaining, turning away, and refraining from wrong livelihood there is at that time — a path factor, included in the path — that is right livelihood at that time. ^1-301

302. What is right effort at that time? Whatever effort in the right way there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is right effort at that time. ^1-302

303. What is right paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention in the right way there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is right paying close attention at that time. ^1-303

304. What is right calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind in the right way there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is right calm focus at that time. ^1-304

305. What is the power of faith at that time? Whatever faith there is at that time — that is the power of faith at that time. ^1-305

306. What is the power of effort at that time? Whatever effort there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-306

307. What is the power of paying close attention at that time? Whatever paying close attention there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the power of paying close attention at that time. ^1-307

308. What is the power of calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is the power of calm focus at that time. ^1-308

310. What is the power of conscience at that time? Whatever conscience about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of conscience at that time. ^1-310

311. What is the power of concern about wrongdoing at that time? Whatever concern about wrongdoing regarding bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of concern about wrongdoing at that time. ^1-311

312. What is non-greed at that time? Whatever absence of greed and non-coveting there is at that time — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-greed at that time. ^1-312

313. What is non-hatred at that time? Whatever absence of hatred and non-ill-will there is at that time — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-hatred at that time. ^1-313

314. What is wisdom at that time? Whatever wisdom that is free from delusion there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is wisdom at that time. ^1-314

315. What is non-coveting at that time? Whatever non-greed there is at that time — that is non-coveting at that time. ^1-315

316. What is non-ill-will at that time? Whatever non-hatred there is at that time — that is non-ill-will at that time. ^1-316

317. What is right view at that time? Whatever wisdom that understands there is at that time — a factor of awakening, a path factor, included in the path — that is right view at that time. ^1-317

318. What is conscience at that time? Whatever conscience about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is conscience at that time. ^1-318

319. What is concern about wrongdoing at that time? Whatever concern about wrongdoing regarding bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is concern about wrongdoing at that time. ^1-319

320. What is calm in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time? Whatever calm in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, and the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions there is at that time — a factor of awakening — that is calm in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time. ^1-320

321. What is calm in the mind at that time? Whatever calm in the aggregate of consciousness there is at that time — a factor of awakening — that is calm in the mind at that time. ^1-321

322. What is lightness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time? 324. What is softness in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time? 326. What is fitness for use in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time? Whatever fitness for use in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, and the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions there is at that time — that is fitness for use in the group of feelings, perceiving, and habits at that time. ^1-326

And at that time there are four groups, two sense bases, two elements, three kinds of food, nine faculties, a five-part state of deep calm in meditation, an eight-part path, seven powers, three roots, one contact, one feeling, one perceiving, one intending, one mind, one aggregate of feeling, one aggregate of perception, one group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, one aggregate of consciousness, one mind base, one faculty of the mind, one mind-consciousness element, one mental-object base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are good for the mind…pe…. ^1-337

#### Twelve Unwholesome States ^1-1-6-0

365. Which things are bad for the mind? At a time when an unwholesome mind has arisen, accompanied by a happy feeling and associated with wrong view, with a visible form, sound, smell, taste, touch, or mental object as object — at that time there is contact, feeling, perceiving, intending, the mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, ease, the mind staying on one thing, the faculty of effort, the faculty of calm focus, the faculty of the mind, the faculty of happy feeling, the faculty of life, wrong view, wrong intention, wrong effort, wrong calm focus, the power of effort, the power of calm focus, the power of lack of conscience, the power of lack of concern about wrongdoing, greed, delusion, coveting, wrong view, lack of conscience, lack of concern about wrongdoing, stilling of the mind, effort, calmness without distraction; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are bad for the mind. ^1-365

366. What is contact at that time? Whatever contact, touching, and being touched there is at that time — that is contact at that time. ^1-366

367. What is feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-367

368. What is perceiving at that time? Whatever perceiving and recognizing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is perceiving at that time. ^1-368

369. What is intending at that time? Whatever intending and willing born of contact with the mind-consciousness element there is at that time — that is intending at that time. ^1-369

370. What is the mind at that time? Whatever mind, heart, and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the mind at that time. ^1-370

371. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? Whatever placing of the mind and wrong intention there is at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-371

372. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? Whatever sustained application and examination of the mind there is at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-372

373. What is a feeling of joy at that time? Whatever joy, gladness, and delight in the mind there is at that time — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-373

374. What is ease at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling born of mind-contact there is at that time — that is ease at that time. ^1-374

375. What is the mind staying on one thing at that time? Whatever steadiness, calm focus, and wrong calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the mind staying on one thing at that time. ^1-375

376. What is the faculty of effort at that time? Whatever starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind there is at that time — that is the faculty of effort at that time. ^1-376

377. What is the faculty of calm focus at that time? Whatever steadiness and calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the faculty of calm focus at that time. ^1-377

378. What is the faculty of the mind at that time? Whatever mind and knowing consciousness there is at that time — that is the faculty of the mind at that time. ^1-378

379. What is the faculty of happy feeling at that time? Whatever pleasant mental feeling there is at that time — that is the faculty of happy feeling at that time. ^1-379

380. What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever life-force that keeps formless things going there is at that time — that is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-380

381. What is wrong view at that time? Whatever wrong belief, wrong theory, and clinging to wrong views there is at that time — that is wrong view at that time. ^1-381

382. What is wrong intention at that time? Whatever putting the mind onto something in the wrong way there is at that time — that is wrong intention at that time. ^1-382

383. What is wrong effort at that time? Whatever effort in the wrong way there is at that time — that is wrong effort at that time. ^1-383

384. What is wrong calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind in the wrong way there is at that time — that is wrong calm focus at that time. ^1-384

385. What is the power of effort at that time? Whatever effort there is at that time — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-385

386. What is the power of calm focus at that time? Whatever calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is the power of calm focus at that time. ^1-386

387. What is the power of lack of conscience at that time? Whatever not feeling conscience when one should, about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of lack of conscience at that time. ^1-387

388. What is the power of lack of concern about wrongdoing at that time? Whatever not feeling concern when one should, about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is the power of lack of concern about wrongdoing at that time. ^1-388

389. What is greed at that time? Whatever greed, wanting, and coveting there is at that time — a root of what is bad for the mind — that is greed at that time. ^1-389

390. What is delusion at that time? Whatever ignorance, confusion, and ignorance there is at that time — a root of what is bad for the mind — that is delusion at that time. ^1-390

391. What is coveting at that time? Whatever greed there is at that time — that is coveting at that time. ^1-391

392. What is wrong view at that time? Whatever wrong belief and clinging to wrong views there is at that time — that is wrong view at that time. ^1-392

393. What is lack of conscience at that time? Whatever not feeling conscience when one should, about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is lack of conscience at that time. ^1-393

394. What is lack of concern about wrongdoing at that time? Whatever not feeling concern when one should, about bad things for the mind there is at that time — that is lack of concern about wrongdoing at that time. ^1-394

395. What is stilling of the mind at that time? Whatever steadiness and calm focus of the mind there is at that time — that is stilling of the mind at that time. ^1-395

396. What is effort at that time? Whatever effort there is at that time — that is effort at that time. ^1-396

397. What is calmness without distraction at that time? Whatever steadiness of the mind without wandering there is at that time — that is calmness without distraction at that time. And whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are bad for the mind.
And at that time there are four groups, two sense bases, two elements, three kinds of food, five faculties, a five-part state of deep calm in meditation, a four-part path, four powers, two roots, one contact…pe… one mental-object base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are bad for the mind…pe…. ^1-397

398. Which is the group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions at that time? Contact, intending, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, the mind staying on one thing, the faculty of effort, the faculty of calm focus, the faculty of life, wrong view, wrong intention, wrong effort, wrong calm focus, the power of effort, the power of calm focus, the power of lack of conscience, the power of lack of concern about wrongdoing, greed, delusion, coveting, wrong view, lack of conscience, lack of concern about wrongdoing, stilling of the mind, effort, calmness without distraction; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions at that time…pe… these things are bad for the mind. ^1-398

#### Indeterminate Results ^1-1-7-0

##### Results of Wholesome Action — Five Consciousnesses ^1-1-7-1-0

431. Which things are neither good nor bad for the mind? When wholesome action of the sense-desire level has been done and accumulated, the result of eye consciousness has arisen, accompanied by calm balance, with a visible form as object — at that time there is contact, feeling, perceiving, intending, the mind, calm balance, the mind staying on one thing, the faculty of the mind, the faculty of equipoise, the faculty of life; and whatever other formless things that arise together at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-431

432. What is contact at that time? Whatever contact, touching, and being touched there is at that time — that is contact at that time. ^1-432

433. What is feeling at that time? Whatever mental feeling born of contact with the eye-consciousness element that is neither pleasant nor unpleasant there is at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-433

434. What is perceiving at that time? Whatever perceiving and recognizing born of contact with the eye-consciousness element there is at that time — that is perceiving at that time. ^1-434

### Book on Materiality ^1-2-0

#### Introduction ^1-2-1-0

583. Which things are neither good nor bad for the mind? Results of things good for the mind and things bad for the mind in the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level that are not included; the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, the aggregate of consciousness; and whatever things are functional — neither good for the mind nor bad for the mind, and not results of action; and all materiality; and the unconditioned element — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-583

#### Outline ^1-2-2-0

##### Singles ^1-2-2-1-0

584. What is all materiality? The four great elements and materiality derived from the four great elements — this is called all materiality. All materiality is not a cause, is without roots, is separate from causes, is conditioned, is put together, is materiality, is of this world, is defiled, can tie the mind down, can knot, can flood, can yoke, can block the mind, can be clung to, can lead to grasping, can stain, is neither good nor bad for the mind, is without an object, is without mental qualities, is separate from the mind, is neither result nor cause of result, is neither stained nor staining, is without putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, is not with only keeping the mind on something, is without putting the mind onto something or keeping the mind on something, is not accompanied by a feeling of joy, is not accompanied by ease, is not accompanied by calm balance, is to be removed neither by insight nor by developing, has causes removed neither by insight nor by developing, leads neither to increase nor to decrease, is for neither those still training nor those who have finished training, is limited, is of the sense-desire level, is not of the form level, is not of the formless level, is included, is not excluded, is not fixed, does not lead out, has arisen, is known by six consciousnesses, is impermanent, is overcome by aging.
Thus the gathering of materiality in one way.
Singles. ^1-584

##### Pairs ^1-2-2-2-0

Two ways of gathering materiality —
There is materiality that is derived, there is materiality that is not derived.
There is materiality that has been taken up, there is materiality that has not been taken up.
There is materiality that has been taken up and can lead to grasping, there is materiality that has not been taken up and can lead to grasping.
There is materiality that can be seen, there is materiality that cannot be seen.
There is materiality that can be pushed against, there is materiality that cannot be pushed against.
There is materiality that is a faculty, there is materiality that is not a faculty.
There is materiality that is a great element, there is materiality that is not a great element.
There is materiality that is expression, there is materiality that is not expression.
There is materiality produced by the mind, there is materiality not produced by the mind.
There is materiality that arises together with the mind, there is materiality that does not arise together with the mind.
There is materiality that follows the mind, there is materiality that does not follow the mind.
There is materiality that is internal, there is materiality that is external.
There is materiality that is coarse, there is materiality that is fine.
There is materiality that is far, there is materiality that is near.
There is materiality that is the basis for eye-contact, there is materiality that is not the basis for eye-contact. There is materiality that is the basis for feeling born of eye-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… eye consciousness, there is materiality that is not the basis for eye consciousness.
There is materiality that is the basis for ear-contact…pe… nose-contact…pe… tongue-contact…pe… body-contact, there is materiality that is not the basis for body-contact. There is materiality that is the basis for feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness, there is materiality that is not the basis for body consciousness.
There is materiality that is the object of eye-contact, there is materiality that is not the object of eye-contact. There is materiality that is the object of feeling born of eye-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… eye consciousness, there is materiality that is not the object of eye consciousness.
There is materiality that is the object of ear-contact…pe… nose-contact…pe… tongue-contact…pe… body-contact, there is materiality that is not the object of body-contact. There is materiality that is the object of feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness, there is materiality that is not the object of body consciousness.
There is materiality that is the eye base, there is materiality that is not the eye base. There is materiality that is the ear base…pe… nose base…pe… tongue base…pe… body base, there is materiality that is not the body base.
There is materiality that is the visible-form base, there is materiality that is not the visible-form base. There is materiality that is the sound base…pe… smell base…pe… taste base…pe… touch base, there is materiality that is not the touch base.
There is materiality that is the eye element, there is materiality that is not the eye element. There is materiality that is the ear element…pe… nose element…pe… tongue element…pe… body element, there is materiality that is not the body element.
There is materiality that is the visible-form element, there is materiality that is not the visible-form element. There is materiality that is the sound element…pe… smell element…pe… taste element…pe… touch element, there is materiality that is not the touch element.
There is materiality that is the eye faculty, there is materiality that is not the eye faculty. There is materiality that is the ear faculty…pe… nose faculty…pe… tongue faculty…pe… body faculty, there is materiality that is not the body faculty.
There is materiality that is the female faculty, there is materiality that is not the female faculty.
There is materiality that is the male faculty, there is materiality that is not the male faculty.
There is materiality that is the life faculty, there is materiality that is not the life faculty.
There is materiality that is bodily expression, there is materiality that is not bodily expression.
There is materiality that is verbal expression, there is materiality that is not verbal expression.
There is materiality that is the space element, there is materiality that is not the space element.
There is materiality that is the water element, there is materiality that is not the water element.
There is materiality that is lightness of materiality, there is materiality that is not lightness of materiality.
There is materiality that is softness of materiality, there is materiality that is not softness of materiality.
There is materiality that is fitness for use of materiality, there is materiality that is not fitness for use of materiality.
There is materiality that is growth of materiality, there is materiality that is not growth of materiality.
There is materiality that is continuity of materiality, there is materiality that is not continuity of materiality.
There is materiality that is aging of materiality, there is materiality that is not aging of materiality.
There is materiality that is impermanence of materiality, there is materiality that is not impermanence of materiality.
There is materiality that is nutriment taken in lumps, there is materiality that is not nutriment taken in lumps.
Thus the gathering of materiality in two ways.
Pairs.

##### Triples ^1-2-2-3-0

Three ways of gathering materiality —
585. Whatever materiality is internal, that is derived. Whatever materiality is external, some is derived, some is not derived.
Whatever materiality is internal, that has been taken up. Whatever materiality is external, some has been taken up, some has not been taken up.
Whatever materiality is internal, that has been taken up and can lead to grasping. Whatever materiality is external, some has been taken up and can lead to grasping, some has not been taken up and can lead to grasping.
Whatever materiality is internal, that cannot be seen. Whatever materiality is external, some can be seen, some cannot be seen.
Whatever materiality is internal, that can be pushed against. Whatever materiality is external, some can be pushed against, some cannot be pushed against.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is a faculty. Whatever materiality is external, some is a faculty, some is not a faculty.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is not a great element. Whatever materiality is external, some is a great element, some is not a great element.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is not expression. Whatever materiality is external, some is expression, some is not expression.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is not produced by the mind. Whatever materiality is external, some is produced by the mind, some is not produced by the mind.
Whatever materiality is internal, that does not arise together with the mind. Whatever materiality is external, some arises together with the mind, some does not arise together with the mind.
Whatever materiality is internal, that does not follow the mind. Whatever materiality is external, some follows the mind, some does not follow the mind.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is coarse. Whatever materiality is external, some is coarse, some is fine.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is near. Whatever materiality is external, some is far, some is near.
Whatever materiality is external, that is not the basis for eye-contact. Whatever materiality is internal, some is the basis for eye-contact, some is not the basis for eye-contact.
Whatever materiality is external, that is not the basis for feeling born of eye-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… eye consciousness. Whatever materiality is internal, some is the basis for eye consciousness, some is not the basis for eye consciousness.
Whatever materiality is external, that is not the basis for ear-contact…pe… nose-contact…pe… tongue-contact…pe… body-contact. Whatever materiality is internal, some is the basis for body-contact, some is not the basis for body-contact. Whatever materiality is external, that is not the basis for feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness. Whatever materiality is internal, some is the basis for body consciousness, some is not the basis for body consciousness.
Whatever materiality is internal, that is not the object of eye-contact. Whatever materiality is external, some is the object of eye-contact, some is not the object of eye-contact. Whatever materiality is derived, some can be pushed against, some cannot be pushed against. Whatever materiality is not derived, some can be pushed against, some cannot be pushed against.
Whatever materiality is derived, some is coarse, some is fine. Whatever materiality is not derived, some is coarse, some is fine.
Whatever materiality is derived, some is far, some is near. Whatever materiality is not derived, some is far, some is near.
Whatever materiality has been taken up, some can be seen, some cannot be seen. Whatever materiality has not been taken up, some can be seen, some cannot be seen.
Whatever materiality has been taken up, some can be pushed against, some cannot be pushed against. Whatever materiality has not been taken up, some can be pushed against, some cannot be pushed against.

Whatever materiality has been taken up and can lead to grasping, some is far, some is near. Whatever materiality has not been taken up and can lead to grasping, some is far, some is near.

#### Analysis of Materiality ^1-2-3-0

##### Single Analysis ^1-2-3-1-0

594. All materiality is not a cause, is without roots, is separate from causes, is conditioned, is put together, is materiality, is of this world, is defiled, can tie the mind down, can knot, can flood, can yoke, can block the mind, can be clung to, can lead to grasping, can stain, is neither good nor bad for the mind, is without an object, is without mental qualities, is separate from the mind, is neither result nor cause of result, is neither stained nor staining, is without putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, is not with only keeping the mind on something, is without putting the mind onto something or keeping the mind on something, is not accompanied by a feeling of joy, is not accompanied by ease, is not accompanied by calm balance, is to be removed neither by insight nor by developing, has causes removed neither by insight nor by developing, leads neither to increase nor to decrease, is for neither those still training nor those who have finished training, is limited, is of the sense-desire level, is not of the form level, is not of the formless level, is included, is not excluded, is not fixed, does not lead out, has arisen, is known by six consciousnesses, is impermanent, is overcome by aging.
Thus the gathering of materiality in one way.
Single Analysis. ^1-594

##### Pair Analysis ^1-2-3-2-0

##### Analysis of Derived Materiality ^1-2-3-3-0

595. Which materiality is derived? The eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, bodily expression, verbal expression, space element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps. ^1-595

596. Which materiality is the eye base? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements, belonging to the body, invisible and able to be pushed against, by which one with an invisible eye that can be pushed against sees visible form that can be seen and pushed against — that is the eye base, eye element, eye faculty, also called the door, the world, the ocean, the white, the field, the basis, the eye, the near shore, the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye base. ^1-596

597. Which materiality is the eye base? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements, belonging to the body, invisible and able to be pushed against, in which visible form that can be seen and pushed against impinges on an invisible eye that can be pushed against — that is the eye base, eye element, eye faculty, also called the door, the world, the ocean, the white, the field, the basis, the eye, the near shore, the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye base. ^1-597

Whatever smell derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against — root smell, heartwood smell, bark smell, leaf smell, flower smell, fruit smell, raw smell, cooked smell, pleasant smell, unpleasant smell, and whatever other smell derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against, depending on which nose-contact arises with smell as basis…pe… depending on which feeling born of nose-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… nose consciousness arises…pe… depending on which nose-contact with a smell as object…pe… depending on which feeling born of nose-contact with a smell as object…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… nose consciousness arises — that is the smell base, smell element — this is that materiality, the smell base. ^1-627

628. Which materiality is the taste base? Whatever taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against — root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, sharp, salty, alkaline, astringent, pleasant taste, unpleasant taste, and whatever other taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against, which one tastes with an invisible tongue that can be pushed against — that is the taste base, taste element — this is that materiality, the taste base. ^1-628

629. Which materiality is the taste base? Whatever taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against — root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, sharp, salty, alkaline, astringent, pleasant taste, unpleasant taste, and whatever other taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against, in which an invisible tongue that can be pushed against impinges on taste that is invisible and able to be pushed against — that is the taste base, taste element — this is that materiality, the taste base. ^1-629

630. Which materiality is the taste base? Whatever taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against — root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, sharp, salty, alkaline, astringent, pleasant taste, unpleasant taste, and whatever other taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against, which as taste that is invisible and able to be pushed against impinges on an invisible tongue that can be pushed against — that is the taste base, taste element — this is that materiality, the taste base. ^1-630

631. Which materiality is the taste base? Whatever taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against — root taste, trunk taste, bark taste, leaf taste, flower taste, fruit taste, sour, sweet, bitter, sharp, salty, alkaline, astringent, pleasant taste, unpleasant taste, and whatever other taste derived from the four great elements that is invisible and able to be pushed against, depending on which tongue-contact arises with taste as basis…pe… depending on which feeling born of tongue-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… tongue consciousness arises…pe… depending on which tongue-contact with a taste as object…pe… depending on which feeling born of tongue-contact with a taste as object…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… tongue consciousness arises — that is the taste base, taste element — this is that materiality, the taste base. ^1-631

632. Which materiality is the female faculty? Whatever marks a woman as female — female sign, female form, female manner, female attire, femaleness, being female — this is that materiality, the female faculty. ^1-632

633. Which materiality is the male faculty? Whatever marks a man as male — male sign, male form, male manner, male attire, maleness, being male — this is that materiality, the male faculty. ^1-633

634. Which materiality is the life faculty? Whatever life-force keeps material things going — this is that materiality, the life faculty. ^1-634

635. Which materiality is bodily expression? Whatever stiffening and holding still of the body when a mind that is good for the mind, bad for the mind, or neither good nor bad for the mind is advancing, retreating, looking straight ahead, looking aside, bending, or stretching — this is that materiality, bodily expression. ^1-635

636. Which materiality is verbal expression? Whatever expression through speech when a mind that is good for the mind, bad for the mind, or neither good nor bad for the mind speaks — this is that materiality, verbal expression. ^1-636

637. Which materiality is the space element? Whatever is space, what has become space, openness, what is open, what is untouched by the four great elements — this is that materiality, the space element. ^1-637

638. Which materiality is lightness of materiality? Whatever lightness and easy change of materiality, not being heavy, not being thick — this is that materiality, lightness of materiality. ^1-638

639. Which materiality is softness of materiality? Whatever softness, pliancy, and not being hard or stiff of materiality — this is that materiality, softness of materiality. ^1-639

640. Which materiality is fitness for use of materiality? Whatever fitness for use and workable state of materiality — this is that materiality, fitness for use of materiality. ^1-640

641. Which materiality is growth of materiality? Whatever gathering together of the sense bases — this is that materiality, growth of materiality. ^1-641

642. Which materiality is continuity of materiality? Whatever growth of materiality — that is continuity of materiality — this is that materiality, continuity of materiality. ^1-642

643. Which materiality is aging of materiality? Whatever aging, wearing out, breaking up, wrinkling, and maturing of the life faculties of materiality — this is that materiality, aging of materiality. ^1-643

644. Which materiality is impermanence of materiality? Whatever breaking up, passing away, destruction, complete breaking up, impermanence, and disappearance of materiality — this is that materiality, impermanence of materiality. ^1-644

645. Which materiality is nutriment taken in lumps? Rice, porridge, barley, fish, meat, milk, curds, butter, ghee, oil, honey, molasses, and whatever other materiality in each country that beings of that country chew with the mouth, swallow, drink down, fill the stomach with, and live on by its nourishment — this is that materiality, nutriment taken in lumps.
This is derived materiality.
Analysis of Derived Materiality.
First Recitation Section in the Book on Materiality. ^1-645

646. Which materiality is not derived? The touch base, the water element. ^1-646

647. Which materiality is the touch base? The earth element, fire element, wind element — hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant contact, painful contact, heavy, light — whatever touch is invisible and able to be pushed against, which one touches with an invisible body that can be pushed against — that is the touch base, touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-647

648. Which materiality is the touch base? The earth element, fire element, wind element — hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant contact, painful contact, heavy, light — whatever touch is invisible and able to be pushed against, in which an invisible body that can be pushed against impinges on what is invisible and able to be pushed against — that is the touch base, touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-648

649. Which materiality is the touch base? The earth element, fire element, wind element — hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant contact, painful contact, heavy, light — whatever touch is invisible and able to be pushed against, which as touch that is invisible and able to be pushed against impinges on an invisible body that can be pushed against — that is the touch base, touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-649

650. Which materiality is the touch base? The earth element, fire element, wind element — hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant contact, painful contact, heavy, light — whatever touch is invisible and able to be pushed against, depending on which body-contact arises with touch as basis…pe… depending on which feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness arises…pe… depending on which body-contact with touch as object…pe… depending on which feeling born of body-contact with touch as object…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness arises — that is the touch base, touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-650

651. Which materiality is the water element? Whatever is water, what has become water, moisture, what has moisture, the binding quality of materiality — this is that materiality, the water element.
This is not derived materiality. ^1-651

652. Which materiality is grasped? The eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped. ^1-652

653. Which materiality is ungrasped? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped. ^1-653

654. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping? The eye base…pe… body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping. ^1-654

655. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping. ^1-655

656. Which materiality is visible? The visible-form base — this is that materiality, visible. ^1-656

657. Which materiality is invisible? The eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, invisible. ^1-657

658. Which materiality is able to be pushed against? The eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, able to be pushed against. ^1-658

659. Which materiality is not able to be pushed against? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not able to be pushed against. ^1-659

660. Which materiality is a faculty? Eye faculty, ear faculty, nose faculty, tongue faculty, body faculty, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty — this is that materiality, a faculty. ^1-660

661. Which materiality is not a faculty? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a faculty. ^1-661

662. Which materiality is a great element? Touch base, water element — this is that materiality, a great element. ^1-662

663. Which materiality is not a great element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a great element. ^1-663

664. Which materiality is expression? Bodily expression, verbal expression — this is that materiality, expression. ^1-664

665. Which materiality is not expression? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not expression. ^1-665

666. Which materiality is arisen from the mind? Bodily expression, verbal expression, and whatever other materiality there is mind-born, with mind as cause, arisen from the mind — visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, arisen from the mind. ^1-666

667. Which materiality is not arisen from the mind? Eye base…pe… body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not mind-born, not with mind as cause, not arisen from the mind — visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not arisen from the mind. ^1-667

668. Which materiality is born together with the mind? Bodily expression, verbal expression — this is that materiality, born together with the mind. ^1-668

669. Which materiality is not born together with the mind? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not born together with the mind. ^1-669

670. Which materiality follows along with the mind? Bodily expression, verbal expression — this is that materiality, follows along with the mind. ^1-670

671. Which materiality does not follow along with the mind? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, does not follow along with the mind. ^1-671

672. Which materiality is internal? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal. ^1-672

673. Which materiality is external? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external. ^1-673

674. Which materiality is coarse? Eye base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, coarse. ^1-674

675. Which materiality is fine? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, fine. ^1-675

676. Which materiality is far? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, far. ^1-676

677. Which materiality is near? Eye base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, near. ^1-677

678. Which materiality is the basis of eye-contact? Eye base — this is that materiality, the basis of eye-contact. ^1-678

679. Which materiality is not the basis of eye-contact? Ear base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the basis of eye-contact. ^1-679

681. Which materiality is not the basis of eye consciousness? Ear base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the basis of eye consciousness. ^1-681

682. Which materiality is the basis of ear-contact…pe… nose-contact…pe… tongue-contact…pe… body-contact? Body base — this is that materiality, the basis of body-contact. ^1-682

683. Which materiality is not the basis of body-contact? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the basis of body-contact. ^1-683

684. Which materiality is the basis of feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness? Body base — this is that materiality, the basis of body consciousness. ^1-684

685. Which materiality is not the basis of body consciousness? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the basis of body consciousness. ^1-685

687. Which materiality is not the object of eye-contact? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the object of eye-contact. ^1-687

688. Which materiality is the object of feeling born of eye-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… eye consciousness? Visible-form base — this is that materiality, the object of eye consciousness. ^1-688

689. Which materiality is not the object of eye consciousness? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the object of eye consciousness. ^1-689

690. Which materiality is the object of ear-contact…pe… nose-contact…pe… tongue-contact…pe… body-contact? Touch base — this is that materiality, the object of body-contact. ^1-690

691. Which materiality is not the object of body-contact? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the object of body-contact. ^1-691

692. Which materiality is the object of feeling born of body-contact…pe… perceiving…pe… intending…pe… body consciousness? Touch base — this is that materiality, the object of body consciousness. ^1-692

693. Which materiality is not the object of body consciousness? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the object of body consciousness. ^1-693

694. Which materiality is the eye base? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye base. ^1-694

695. Which materiality is not the eye base? Ear base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the eye base. ^1-695

696. Which materiality is the ear base…pe… nose base…pe… tongue base…pe… body base? Whatever is the body — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the body base. ^1-696

697. Which materiality is not the body base? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the body base. ^1-697

698. Which materiality is the visible-form base? Whatever visible form derived from the four great elements that has the appearance of color…pe… visible-form element — this is that materiality, the visible-form base. ^1-698

699. Which materiality is not the visible-form base? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the visible-form base. ^1-699

700. Which materiality is the sound base…pe… smell base…pe… taste base…pe… touch base? Earth element…pe… touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-700

701. Which materiality is not the touch base? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the touch base. ^1-701

702. Which materiality is the eye element? Eye base — this is that materiality, the eye element. ^1-702

703. Which materiality is not the eye element? Ear base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the eye element. ^1-703

704. Which materiality is the ear element…pe… nose element…pe… tongue element…pe… body element? Body base — this is that materiality, the body element. ^1-704

705. Which materiality is not the body element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the body element. ^1-705

706. Which materiality is the visible-form element? Visible-form base — this is that materiality, the visible-form element. ^1-706

707. Which materiality is not the visible-form element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the visible-form element. ^1-707

708. Which materiality is the sound element…pe… smell element…pe… taste element…pe… touch element? Touch base — this is that materiality, the touch element. ^1-708

709. Which materiality is not the touch element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the touch element. ^1-709

710. Which materiality is the eye faculty? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye faculty. ^1-710

711. Which materiality is not the eye faculty? Ear base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the eye faculty. ^1-711

712. Which materiality is the ear faculty…pe… nose faculty…pe… tongue faculty…pe… body faculty? Whatever is the body — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the body faculty. ^1-712

713. Which materiality is not the body faculty? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the body faculty. ^1-713

714. Which materiality is the female faculty? Whatever marks a woman as female — female sign, female form, female manner, female attire, femaleness, being female — this is that materiality, the female faculty. ^1-714

715. Which materiality is not the female faculty? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the female faculty. ^1-715

716. Which materiality is the male faculty? Whatever marks a man as male — male sign, male form, male manner, male attire, maleness, being male — this is that materiality, the male faculty. ^1-716

717. Which materiality is not the male faculty? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the male faculty. ^1-717

718. Which materiality is the life faculty? Whatever life-span, staying, keeping going, maintaining, moving, carrying on, protecting, life, life faculty of those material things — this is that materiality, the life faculty. ^1-718

719. Which materiality is not the life faculty? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the life faculty. ^1-719

720. Which materiality is bodily expression? Whatever stiffening, holding still, and held-still state of the body when a mind that is good for the mind, bad for the mind, or neither good nor bad for the mind is advancing, retreating, looking straight ahead, looking aside, bending, or stretching — expression, making known, made-known state — this is that materiality, bodily expression. ^1-720

721. Which materiality is not bodily expression? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not bodily expression. ^1-721

722. Which materiality is verbal expression? Whatever speech, voice, spoken words, utterance, sound, sound-making, speech, verbal division — this is called speech. Whatever expression, making known, made-known state through that speech — this is that materiality, verbal expression. ^1-722

723. Which materiality is not verbal expression? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not verbal expression. ^1-723

724. Which materiality is the space element? Whatever is space, what has become space, openness, what is open, what is untouched by the four great elements — this is that materiality, the space element. ^1-724

725. Which materiality is not the space element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the space element. ^1-725

726. Which materiality is the water element? Whatever is water, what has become water, moisture, what has moisture, the binding quality of materiality — this is that materiality, the water element. ^1-726

727. Which materiality is not the water element? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not the water element. ^1-727

728. Which materiality is lightness of materiality? Whatever lightness, easy change, not being heavy, not being thick of materiality — this is that materiality, lightness of materiality. ^1-728

729. Which materiality is not lightness of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not lightness of materiality. ^1-729

730. Which materiality is softness of materiality? Whatever softness, pliancy, not being hard, not being stiff of materiality — this is that materiality, softness of materiality. ^1-730

731. Which materiality is not softness of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not softness of materiality. ^1-731

732. Which materiality is fitness for use of materiality? Whatever fitness for use, workable state, workability of materiality — this is that materiality, fitness for use of materiality. ^1-732

733. Which materiality is not fitness for use of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not fitness for use of materiality. ^1-733

734. Which materiality is growth of materiality? Whatever gathering together of the sense bases — that is growth of materiality — this is that materiality, growth of materiality. ^1-734

735. Which materiality is not growth of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not growth of materiality. ^1-735

736. Which materiality is continuity of materiality? Whatever growth of materiality — that is continuity of materiality — this is that materiality, continuity of materiality. ^1-736

737. Which materiality is not continuity of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not continuity of materiality. ^1-737

738. Which materiality is aging of materiality? Whatever aging, wearing out, breaking up, wrinkling, maturing of the life faculties of materiality — this is that materiality, aging of materiality. ^1-738

739. Which materiality is not aging of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not aging of materiality. ^1-739

740. Which materiality is impermanence of materiality? Whatever breaking up, passing away, destruction, complete breaking up, impermanence, disappearance of materiality — this is that materiality, impermanence of materiality. ^1-740

741. Which materiality is not impermanence of materiality? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not impermanence of materiality. ^1-741

742. Which materiality is nutriment taken in lumps? Rice, porridge, barley, fish, meat, milk, curds, butter, ghee, oil, honey, molasses, and whatever other materiality in each country that beings of that country chew with the mouth, use for tooth-cleaning, swallow, drink down, fill the stomach with, and live on by its nourishment — this is that materiality, nutriment taken in lumps. ^1-742

743. Which materiality is not nutriment taken in lumps? Eye base…pe… impermanence of materiality — this is that materiality, not nutriment taken in lumps.
Thus the gathering of materiality in two ways.
Pair Analysis. ^1-743

##### Triple Analysis ^1-2-3-4-0

744. Which materiality is internal and derived? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and derived. ^1-744

745. Which materiality is external and derived? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and derived. ^1-745

746. Which materiality is external and not derived? Touch base, water element — this is that materiality, external and not derived. ^1-746

747. Which materiality is internal and grasped? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and grasped. ^1-747

748. Which materiality is external and grasped? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and grasped. ^1-748

749. Which materiality is external and ungrasped? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and ungrasped. ^1-749

750. Which materiality is internal and grasped and leading to grasping? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and grasped and leading to grasping. ^1-750

751. Which materiality is external and grasped and leading to grasping? 752. Which materiality is external and ungrasped and not leading to grasping? 753. Which materiality is internal and invisible? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and invisible. ^1-753

754. Which materiality is external and visible? Visible-form base — this is that materiality, external and visible. ^1-754

755. Which materiality is external and invisible? Sound base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and invisible. ^1-755

757. Which materiality is external and able to be pushed against? Visible-form base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, external and able to be pushed against. ^1-757

758. Which materiality is external and not able to be pushed against? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and not able to be pushed against. ^1-758

760. Which materiality is external and a faculty? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty — this is that materiality, external and a faculty. ^1-760

761. Which materiality is external and not a faculty? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and not a faculty. ^1-761

764. Which materiality is external and not a great element? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and not a great element. ^1-764

765. Which materiality is internal and not expression? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and not expression. ^1-765

766. Which materiality is external and expression? Bodily expression, verbal expression — this is that materiality, external and expression. ^1-766

767. Which materiality is external and not expression? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and not expression. ^1-767

768. Which materiality is internal and not arisen from the mind? Eye base…pe… body base — this is that materiality, internal and not arisen from the mind. ^1-768

769. Which materiality is external and arisen from the mind? Bodily expression, verbal expression, and whatever other materiality there is mind-born, with mind as cause, arisen from the mind — visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and arisen from the mind. ^1-769

770. Which materiality is external and not arisen from the mind? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not mind-born, not with mind as cause, not arisen from the mind — visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, external and not arisen from the mind. ^1-770
