880. Which materiality is external and nutriment taken in lumps? Rice, porridge, barley, fish, meat, milk, curds, butter, ghee, oil, honey, molasses, and whatever other materiality in each country that beings of that country chew with the mouth, use for tooth-cleaning, swallow, drink down, fill the stomach with, and live on by its nourishment — this is that materiality, external and nutriment taken in lumps. ^1-880

881. Which materiality is external and not nutriment taken in lumps? Visible-form base…pe… impermanence of materiality — this is that materiality, external and not nutriment taken in lumps.
Thus the gathering of materiality in three ways.
Triple Analysis. ^1-881

##### Quadruple Analysis ^1-2-3-5-0

882. Which materiality is derived and grasped? Eye base…pe… body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, space element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, derived and grasped. ^1-882

883. Which materiality is derived and ungrasped? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, space element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, derived and ungrasped. ^1-883

884. Which materiality is not derived and grasped? Touch base produced by action, water element — this is that materiality, not derived and grasped. ^1-884

885. Which materiality is not derived and ungrasped? Touch base not produced by action, water element — this is that materiality, not derived and ungrasped. ^1-885

886. Which materiality is derived and grasped and leading to grasping? 918. Which materiality is grasped and far? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and far. ^1-918

919. Which materiality is grasped and near? Eye base…pe… body base, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, grasped and near. ^1-919

920. Which materiality is ungrasped and far? Bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and far. ^1-920

921. Which materiality is ungrasped and near? Sound base, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, ungrasped and near. ^1-921

922. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and visible? Visible-form base produced by action — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and visible. ^1-922

923. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and invisible? Eye base…pe… body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and invisible. ^1-923

924. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and visible? Visible-form base not produced by action — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and visible. ^1-924

925. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and invisible? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and invisible. ^1-925

926. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and able to be pushed against? Eye base…pe… body base, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and able to be pushed against. ^1-926

927. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and not able to be pushed against? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and not able to be pushed against. ^1-927

928. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and able to be pushed against? Sound base, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and able to be pushed against. ^1-928

929. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and not able to be pushed against? Bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and not able to be pushed against. ^1-929

930. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and a great element? Touch base produced by action, water element — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and a great element. ^1-930

931. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and not a great element? Eye base…pe… body base, female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, space element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and not a great element. ^1-931

932. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and a great element? Touch base not produced by action, water element — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and a great element. ^1-932

933. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and not a great element? Sound base, bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, space element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and not a great element. ^1-933

934. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and coarse? Eye base…pe… body base, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and coarse. ^1-934

935. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and fine? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and fine. ^1-935

936. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and coarse? Sound base, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and coarse. ^1-936

937. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and fine? Bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and fine. ^1-937

938. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and far? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and far. ^1-938

939. Which materiality is grasped and leading to grasping and near? Eye base…pe… body base, and whatever other materiality there is produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, grasped and leading to grasping and near. ^1-939

940. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and far? Bodily expression, verbal expression, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, fitness for use of materiality, aging of materiality, impermanence of materiality, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — space element, water element, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and far. ^1-940

941. Which materiality is ungrasped and not leading to grasping and near? Sound base, and whatever other materiality there is not produced by action — visible-form base, smell base, taste base, touch base — this is that materiality, ungrasped and not leading to grasping and near. ^1-941

942. Which materiality is able to be pushed against and a faculty? Eye faculty…pe… body faculty — this is that materiality, able to be pushed against and a faculty. ^1-942

943. Which materiality is able to be pushed against and not a faculty? Visible-form base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, able to be pushed against and not a faculty. ^1-943

944. Which materiality is not able to be pushed against and a faculty? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty — this is that materiality, not able to be pushed against and a faculty. ^1-944

945. Which materiality is not able to be pushed against and not a faculty? Bodily expression, verbal expression…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not able to be pushed against and not a faculty. ^1-945

946. Which materiality is able to be pushed against and a great element? Touch base — this is that materiality, able to be pushed against and a great element. ^1-946

947. Which materiality is able to be pushed against and not a great element? Eye base…pe… taste base — this is that materiality, able to be pushed against and not a great element. ^1-947

948. Which materiality is not able to be pushed against and a great element? Water element — this is that materiality, not able to be pushed against and a great element. ^1-948

949. Which materiality is not able to be pushed against and not a great element? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not able to be pushed against and not a great element. ^1-949

950. Which materiality is a faculty and coarse? Eye faculty…pe… body faculty — this is that materiality, a faculty and coarse. ^1-950

951. Which materiality is a faculty and fine? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty — this is that materiality, a faculty and fine. ^1-951

952. Which materiality is not a faculty and coarse? Visible-form base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, not a faculty and coarse. ^1-952

953. Which materiality is not a faculty and fine? Bodily expression, verbal expression…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a faculty and fine. ^1-953

954. Which materiality is a faculty and far? Female faculty, male faculty, life faculty — this is that materiality, a faculty and far. ^1-954

955. Which materiality is a faculty and near? Eye faculty…pe… body faculty — this is that materiality, a faculty and near. ^1-955

956. Which materiality is not a faculty and far? Bodily expression, verbal expression…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a faculty and far. ^1-956

957. Which materiality is not a faculty and near? Visible-form base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, not a faculty and near. ^1-957

958. Which materiality is a great element and coarse? Touch base — this is that materiality, a great element and coarse. ^1-958

959. Which materiality is a great element and fine? Water element — this is that materiality, a great element and fine. ^1-959

960. Which materiality is not a great element and coarse? Eye base…pe… taste base — this is that materiality, not a great element and coarse. ^1-960

961. Which materiality is not a great element and fine? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a great element and fine. ^1-961

962. Which materiality is a great element and far? Water element — this is that materiality, a great element and far. ^1-962

963. Which materiality is a great element and near? Touch base — this is that materiality, a great element and near. ^1-963

964. Which materiality is not a great element and far? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a great element and far. ^1-964

965. Which materiality is not a great element and near? Eye base…pe… taste base — this is that materiality, not a great element and near. ^1-965

966. Visible-form base is seen, sound base is heard, smell base, taste base, and touch base are sensed, all materiality is materiality known by the mind.
Thus the gathering of materiality in four ways.
Quadruple Analysis. ^1-966

##### Fivefold Analysis ^1-2-3-6-0

967. Which materiality is the earth element? Whatever is hard, rough quality, hardness, hard state, internal or external, grasped or ungrasped — this is that materiality, the earth element. ^1-967

968. Which materiality is the water element? Whatever is water, what has become water, moisture, what has moisture, the binding quality of materiality, internal or external, grasped or ungrasped — this is that materiality, the water element. ^1-968

969. Which materiality is the fire element? Whatever is fire, what has become fire, heat, what has heat, warmth, what has warmth, internal or external, grasped or ungrasped — this is that materiality, the fire element. ^1-969

970. Which materiality is the wind element? Whatever is wind, what has become wind, stiffening quality of materiality, internal or external, grasped or ungrasped — this is that materiality, the wind element. ^1-970

971. Which materiality is derived? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, derived.
Thus the gathering of materiality in five ways.
Fivefold Analysis. ^1-971

##### Sixfold Analysis ^1-2-3-7-0

972. Visible-form base is materiality known by eye consciousness, sound base is materiality known by ear consciousness, smell base is materiality known by nose consciousness, taste base is materiality known by tongue consciousness, touch base is materiality known by body consciousness, all materiality is materiality known by mind consciousness.
Thus the gathering of materiality in six ways.
Sixfold Analysis. ^1-972

##### Sevenfold Analysis ^1-2-3-8-0

973. Visible-form base is materiality known by eye consciousness, sound base is materiality known by ear consciousness, smell base is materiality known by nose consciousness, taste base is materiality known by tongue consciousness, touch base is materiality known by body consciousness, visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, and touch base are materiality known by mind-element consciousness, all materiality is materiality known by mind-consciousness-element consciousness.
Thus the gathering of materiality in seven ways.
Sevenfold Analysis. ^1-973

##### Eightfold Analysis ^1-2-3-9-0

974. Visible-form base is materiality known by eye consciousness, sound base is materiality known by ear consciousness, smell base is materiality known by nose consciousness, taste base is materiality known by tongue consciousness, pleasant touch with pleasant contact is materiality known by body consciousness, unpleasant touch with painful contact is materiality known by body consciousness, visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, and touch base are materiality known by mind-element consciousness, all materiality is materiality known by mind-consciousness-element consciousness.
Thus the gathering of materiality in eight ways.
Eightfold Analysis. ^1-974

##### Ninefold Analysis ^1-2-3-10-0

975. Which materiality is the eye faculty? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye faculty. ^1-975

976. Which materiality is the ear faculty…pe… nose faculty…pe… tongue faculty…pe… body faculty…pe… female faculty…pe… male faculty…pe… life faculty? Whatever life-span, staying, keeping going, maintaining, moving, carrying on, protecting, life, life faculty of those material things — this is that materiality, the life faculty. ^1-976

977. Which materiality is not a faculty? Visible-form base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a faculty.
Thus the gathering of materiality in nine ways.
Ninefold Analysis. ^1-977

##### Tenfold Analysis ^1-2-3-11-0

978. Which materiality is the eye faculty? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye faculty. ^1-978

979. Which materiality is the ear faculty…pe… nose faculty…pe… tongue faculty…pe… body faculty…pe… female faculty…pe… male faculty…pe… life faculty? Whatever life-span, staying, keeping going, maintaining, moving, carrying on, protecting, life, life faculty of those material things — this is that materiality, the life faculty. ^1-979

980. Which materiality is not a faculty and able to be pushed against? Visible-form base…pe… touch base — this is that materiality, not a faculty and able to be pushed against. ^1-980

981. Which materiality is not a faculty and not able to be pushed against? Bodily expression…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, not a faculty and not able to be pushed against.
Thus the gathering of materiality in ten ways.
Tenfold Analysis. ^1-981

##### Elevenfold Analysis ^1-2-3-12-0

982. Which materiality is the eye base? Whatever is the eye — the sensitive part derived from the four great elements…pe… also called the empty village — this is that materiality, the eye base. ^1-982

983. Which materiality is the ear base…pe… nose base…pe… tongue base…pe… body base…pe… visible-form base…pe… sound base…pe… smell base…pe… taste base…pe… touch base? Earth element…pe… touch element — this is that materiality, the touch base. ^1-983

984. Which materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base? Female faculty…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — this is that materiality, invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base.
Thus the gathering of materiality in eleven ways.
Elevenfold Analysis.
Eighth Recitation Section.
Analysis of Materiality.
Book on Materiality Finished. ^1-984

### Book on Summary ^1-3-0

#### Triple Summary ^1-3-1-0

985. Which things are good for the mind? The three roots of what is good for the mind — non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion; the aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are good for the mind. ^1-985

986. Which things are bad for the mind? The three roots of what is bad for the mind — greed, hatred, delusion; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are bad for the mind. ^1-986

987. Which things are neither good nor bad for the mind? Results of things good for the mind and bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness; things that are functional, neither good for the mind nor bad for the mind, nor results of action; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-987

988. Which things are associated with a happy feeling? At the sense-desire level, form level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of happy feeling, except happy feeling itself; the aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are associated with a happy feeling. ^1-988

989. Which things are associated with a painful feeling? At the sense-desire level in the sphere of painful feeling, except painful feeling itself; the aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are associated with a painful feeling. ^1-989

990. Which things are associated with calm balance? At the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of calm balance, except calm balance itself; the aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are associated with calm balance. ^1-990

991. Which things are results? Results of things good for the mind and bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are results. ^1-991

992. Which things are both result and things that produce result? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are both result and things that produce result. ^1-992

993. Which things are neither result nor things that produce result? Things that are functional, neither good for the mind nor bad for the mind, nor results of action; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are neither result nor things that produce result. ^1-993

994. Which things are grasped and leading to grasping? Results of defiled things good for the mind and bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; whatever materiality is produced by action — these things are grasped and leading to grasping. ^1-994

995. Which things are ungrasped and not leading to grasping? Things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; things that are functional, neither good for the mind nor bad for the mind, nor results of action; whatever materiality is not produced by action — these things are ungrasped and not leading to grasping. ^1-995

996. Which things are ungrasped and not leading to grasping in the supra-mundane sense? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are ungrasped and not leading to grasping in the supra-mundane sense. ^1-996

997. Which things are stained and staining? The three roots of what is bad for the mind — greed, hatred, delusion; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are stained and staining. ^1-997

998. Which things are unstained but staining? Things good for the mind, neither good nor bad for the mind, defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality, aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness — these things are unstained but staining. ^1-998

999. Which things are unstained and not staining? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are unstained and not staining. ^1-999

1000. Which things have putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something? At the sense-desire level, form level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, except putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something themselves; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something. ^1-1000

1001. Which things have only keeping the mind on something? At the form level and supra-mundane level in the sphere of only keeping the mind on something, except keeping the mind on something itself; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have only keeping the mind on something. ^1-1001

1002. Which things have neither putting the mind onto something nor keeping the mind on something? At the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere without putting the mind onto something or keeping the mind on something; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things have neither putting the mind onto something nor keeping the mind on something. ^1-1002

1003. Which things are accompanied by a feeling of joy? At the sense-desire level, form level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of joy, except joy itself; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1003

1004. Which things are accompanied by ease? At the sense-desire level, form level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of ease, except ease itself; the aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are accompanied by ease. ^1-1004

1005. Which things are accompanied by calm balance? At the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level in the sphere of calm balance, except calm balance itself; the aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things are accompanied by calm balance. ^1-1005

1006. Which things are to be removed by insight? The three things that tie the mind down — personality view, doubt, taking virtue and observances as sufficient. ^1-1006

1007. What is personality view? Here an untaught ordinary person who has not seen the noble ones, is unskilled and untrained in the noble teaching, has not seen good persons, is unskilled and untrained in the teaching of good persons — regards form as a self, or a self as possessing form, or form as in a self, or a self as in form; regards feeling as a self…pe… perceiving…pe… habits…pe… consciousness as a self, or a self as possessing consciousness, or consciousness as in a self, or a self as in consciousness. Whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view, deep view, view as a thorn, view as distortion, view as agitation, thing that ties the mind down — view, grasping, taking up, settling into, holding to, wrong path, wrong way, wrongness, sectarian standpoint, upside-down grasping — this is called personality view. ^1-1007

1008. What is doubt? Doubt and wavering about the Teacher, about the teaching, about the community, about the training, about the past, about the future, about past and future together, about things that arise dependently with conditions as basis. Whatever wavering of this kind — doubting, state of doubting, confusion, doubt, being of two minds, double path, uncertainty, not taking one side, hesitation, trembling, not going all the way through, stiffness of mind, crookedness of mind — this is called doubt. ^1-1008

1009. What is taking virtue and observances as sufficient? The view of outside ascetics and brahmins that "purity comes from virtue, purity comes from observance, purity comes from virtue and observance." Whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view, deep view, view as a thorn, view as distortion, view as agitation, thing that ties the mind down — view, grasping, taking up, settling into, holding to, wrong path, wrong way, wrongness, sectarian standpoint, upside-down grasping — this is called taking virtue and observances as sufficient. ^1-1009

1010. These three things that tie the mind down; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are to be removed by insight. ^1-1010

1011. Which things are to be removed by developing? The remaining greed, hatred, and delusion; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are to be removed by developing. ^1-1011

1012. Which things are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing? Things good for the mind, neither good nor bad for the mind, at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-1012

1013. Which things have causes that are to be removed by insight? The three things that tie the mind down — personality view, doubt, taking virtue and observances as sufficient. ^1-1013

1014. What is personality view?…pe… this is called personality view. ^1-1014

1015. What is doubt?…pe… this is called doubt. ^1-1015

1016. What is taking virtue and observances as sufficient?…pe… this is called taking virtue and observances as sufficient. ^1-1016

1017. These three things that tie the mind down; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things have causes that are to be removed by insight. The three things that tie the mind down — personality view, doubt, taking virtue and observances as sufficient — these things are to be removed by insight. Greed, hatred, and delusion of the same kind — these things are causes that are to be removed by insight. Harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things have causes that are to be removed by insight. ^1-1017

1018. Which things have causes that are to be removed by developing? The remaining greed, hatred, and delusion — these things are causes that are to be removed by developing. Harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things have causes that are to be removed by developing. ^1-1018

1019. Which things have causes that are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things have causes that are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-1019

1020. Which things lead to increase? Things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things lead to increase. ^1-1020

1021. Which things lead to decrease? The four supra-mundane paths — these things lead to decrease. ^1-1021

1022. Which things lead neither to increase nor to decrease? Results of things good for the mind and bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; things that are functional, neither good for the mind nor bad for the mind, nor results of action; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things lead neither to increase nor to decrease. ^1-1022

1023. Which things belong to those still training? The four supra-mundane paths and the three lower noble fruits — these things belong to those still training. ^1-1023

1024. Which things belong to those who have finished training? The highest fruit of being an arahant — these things belong to those who have finished training. ^1-1024

1025. Which things belong neither to those still training nor to those who have finished training? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things belong neither to those still training nor to those who have finished training. ^1-1025

1026. Which things are limited? All things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are limited. ^1-1026

1027. Which things are lofty? Things good for the mind and neither good nor bad for the mind at the form level and formless level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are lofty. ^1-1027

1028. Which things are limitless? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are limitless. ^1-1028

1029. Which things have a limited object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on limited things — these things have a limited object. ^1-1029

1030. Which things have a lofty object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on lofty things — these things have a lofty object. ^1-1030

1031. Which things have a limitless object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on limitless things — these things have a limitless object. ^1-1031

1032. Which things are low? The three roots of what is bad for the mind — greed, hatred, delusion; harmful habits of mind of the same kind; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them; bodily action, verbal action, and mental action arising from them — these things are low. ^1-1032

1033. Which things are middle? Things good for the mind, neither good nor bad for the mind, defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are middle. ^1-1033

1034. Which things are excellent? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are excellent. ^1-1034

1035. Which things are fixed in wrongness? The five actions with immediate result, and whatever is fixed in wrong view — these things are fixed in wrongness. ^1-1035

1036. Which things are fixed in rightness? The four supra-mundane paths — these things are fixed in rightness. ^1-1036

1037. Which things are not fixed? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are not fixed. ^1-1037

1038. Which things have the path as object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on the noble path — these things have the path as object. ^1-1038

1039. Which things have the path as cause? For one who shares in the noble path, except the path factors; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have the path as cause. For one who shares in the noble path, right view is both path and cause; except right view; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have the path as cause. For one who shares in the noble path, non-greed, non-hatred, and non-delusion — these things are causes of the path. The aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have the path as cause. ^1-1039

1040. Which things have the path as chief? Mind and mental qualities that arise making the noble path chief. For one who shares in the noble path and develops the path with investigation as chief, except investigation; the aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness associated with them — these things have the path as chief. ^1-1040

1041. Which things have arisen? Whatever things are born, become, arisen, produced, come to be, appeared, arisen, come into being, stood up, come forth, arisen, included as arisen — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things have arisen. ^1-1041

1042. Which things have not arisen? Whatever things are unborn, not become, not arisen, not produced, not come to be, not appeared, not arisen, not come into being, not stood up, not come forth, not arisen, included as not arisen — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things have not arisen. ^1-1042

1043. Which things are productive? Results of things good for the mind and bad for the mind that are not yet matured at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; whatever materiality produced by action will arise — these things are productive. ^1-1043

1044. Which things are past? Whatever things are past, ceased, gone, changed, come to an end, disappeared, having arisen and gone, past, included as past — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things are past. ^1-1044

1045. Which things are future? Whatever things are unborn, not become, not arisen, not produced, not come to be, not appeared, not arisen, not come into being, not stood up, not come forth, future, included as future — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things are future. ^1-1045

1046. Which things are present? Whatever things are born, become, arisen, produced, come to be, appeared, arisen, come into being, stood up, come forth, present, included as present — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things are present. ^1-1046

1047. Which things have a past object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on past things — these things have a past object. ^1-1047

1048. Which things have a future object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on future things — these things have a future object. ^1-1048

1049. Which things have a present object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on present things — these things have a present object. ^1-1049

1050. Which things are internal? Whatever things belonging to each being are inward, personal, one's own, individual, grasped — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things are internal. ^1-1050

1051. Which things are external? Whatever things belonging to each other being and other person are inward, personal, one's own, individual, grasped — materiality, feeling, perceiving, habits, consciousness — these things are external. ^1-1051

1052. Which things are both internal and external? Both together — these things are both internal and external. ^1-1052

1053. Which things have an internal object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on internal things — these things have an internal object. ^1-1053

1054. Which things have an external object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on external things — these things have an external object. ^1-1054

1055. Which things have an internal-and-external object? Mind and mental qualities that arise depending on internal-and-external things — these things have an internal-and-external object. ^1-1055

1056. Which things are visible and able to be pushed against? Visible-form base — these things are visible and able to be pushed against. ^1-1056

1057. Which things are invisible and able to be pushed against? Eye base, ear base, nose base, tongue base, body base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base — these things are invisible and able to be pushed against. ^1-1057

1058. Which things are invisible and not able to be pushed against? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness; whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base; the unconditioned element — these things are invisible and not able to be pushed against.
Triple Summary. ^1-1058

#### Pair Summary ^1-3-2-0

##### Group on Causes ^1-3-2-1-0

1059. Which things are causes? Three wholesome causes, three unwholesome causes, three indeterminate causes, nine sense-desire-level causes, six form-level causes, six formless-level causes, six supra-mundane causes. ^1-1059

1060. What are the three wholesome causes? Non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion. ^1-1060

1061. What is non-greed? Whatever is non-greed, not being greedy, state of not being greedy, absence of passion, not being passionate, state of not being passionate, absence of coveting, non-greed as a root of what is good for the mind — this is called non-greed. ^1-1061

1062. What is non-hatred? Whatever is non-hatred, not hating, state of not hating, loving-kindness, being kind, state of being kind, not being hostile, not being hostile in action, state of not being hostile, wishing well, compassion, absence of ill-will, absence of injury, non-hatred as a root of what is good for the mind — this is called non-hatred. ^1-1062

1063. What is non-delusion? Knowledge of suffering, knowledge of the origin of suffering, knowledge of the cessation of suffering, knowledge of the way leading to the cessation of suffering, knowledge of the past, knowledge of the future, knowledge of past and future together, knowledge of things that arise dependently with conditions as basis; whatever wisdom of this kind — understanding, knowing, examining, thorough examining, examining things, marking, observing, clearly observing, intelligence, skill, sharpness, breadth, thinking, investigating, wide wisdom, guiding wisdom, insight, clear comprehension, prompting, wisdom, faculty of wisdom, power of wisdom, sword of wisdom, fortress of wisdom, light of wisdom, radiance of wisdom, jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, examining things, right view — this is called non-delusion.
These are the three wholesome causes. ^1-1063

1064. What are the three unwholesome causes? Greed, hatred, delusion. ^1-1064

1065. What is greed? Whatever is passion, being passionate, inclination, favoring, delight, delight in passion, passion of mind, desire, confusion, clinging, greed, thick greed, attachment, mud, stickiness, fever, deceit, what gives birth, what ties, what weaves, what nets, what flows, what sticks, what spreads, what pulls along, second wish, plan, stream of becoming, forest, thicket, entanglement, affection, expectation, bond, hope, wishing, state of wishing, hope for visible form, hope for sound, hope for smell, hope for taste, hope for touch, hope for gain, hope for wealth, hope for children, hope for life, muttering, repeated muttering, intense muttering, muttering, state of muttering, greediness, being greedy, state of being greedy, being bent on gain, wanting what is good improperly, wrong passion, uneven greed, bending toward, wishing, longing, aspiring, craving for sense pleasures, craving for becoming, craving for non-becoming, craving for form, craving for formlessness, craving for cessation, craving for visible form, craving for sound, craving for smell, craving for taste, craving for touch, craving for mental objects, flood, yoke, knot, grasping, obstruction, hindrance, covering, bond, stain, underlying tendency, outbreak, creeper, dissatisfaction, root of suffering, source of suffering, origin of suffering, snare of Māra, bait of Māra, domain of Māra, river of craving, net of craving, rope of craving, ocean of craving, coveting, greed as a root of what is bad for the mind — this is called greed. ^1-1065

1066. What is hatred? "He did me wrong" — hatred arises; "he does me wrong" — hatred arises; "he will do me wrong" — hatred arises; "he did wrong to what is dear and pleasing to me"…pe… "he does wrong"…pe… "he will do wrong" — hatred arises; "he did good to what is disliked and displeasing to me"…pe… "he does good"…pe… "he will do good" — hatred arises; or hatred arises where there is no reason. Whatever hatred of this kind in the mind — hatred, resistance, opposing, anger, angry state, full anger, hatred, hateful state, full hatred, crookedness of mind, ill-will of mind, hatred, anger, being angry, state of being angry, hatred, hating, state of hating, ill-will, injuring, state of injuring, opposition, resisting, fierceness, being like an enemy, displeasure of mind — this is called hatred. ^1-1066

1067. What is delusion? Not knowing suffering, not knowing the origin of suffering, not knowing the cessation of suffering, not knowing the way leading to the cessation of suffering, not knowing the past, not knowing the future, not knowing past and future together, not knowing things that arise dependently with conditions as basis; whatever not-knowing of this kind — ignorance, not seeing, not understanding, not penetrating, not realizing, not taking in, not going all the way through, not looking at, not reviewing, not looking back, dullness, foolishness, lack of clear comprehension, delusion, full delusion, complete delusion, ignorance, flood of ignorance, yoke of ignorance, underlying tendency of ignorance, outbreak of ignorance, banner of ignorance, delusion as a root of what is bad for the mind — this is called delusion.
These are the three unwholesome causes. ^1-1067

1068. What are the three indeterminate causes? From results of things good for the mind, or from functional indeterminate things — non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion — these are the three indeterminate causes. ^1-1068

1069. What are the nine sense-desire-level causes? Three wholesome causes, three unwholesome causes, three indeterminate causes — these are the nine sense-desire-level causes. ^1-1069

1070. What are the six form-level causes? Three wholesome causes, three indeterminate causes — these are the six form-level causes. ^1-1070

1071. What are the six formless-level causes? Three wholesome causes, three indeterminate causes — these are the six formless-level causes. ^1-1071

1072. What are the six supra-mundane causes? Three wholesome causes, three indeterminate causes — these are the six supra-mundane causes. ^1-1072

1073. What are the three wholesome causes? Non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion. ^1-1073

1074. What is non-greed? Whatever is non-greed, not being greedy, state of not being greedy, absence of passion, not being passionate, state of not being passionate, absence of coveting, non-greed as a root of what is good for the mind — this is called non-greed. ^1-1074

1075. What is non-hatred? Whatever is non-hatred, not hating, state of not hating…pe… absence of ill-will, absence of injury, non-hatred as a root of what is good for the mind — this is called non-hatred. ^1-1075

1076. What is non-delusion? Knowledge of suffering, knowledge of the origin of suffering, knowledge of the cessation of suffering, knowledge of the way leading to the cessation of suffering, knowledge of the past, knowledge of the future, knowledge of past and future together, knowledge of things that arise dependently with conditions as basis; whatever wisdom of this kind — understanding, knowing, examining, thorough examining, examining things, marking, observing, clearly observing, intelligence, skill, sharpness, breadth, thinking, investigating, wide wisdom, guiding wisdom, insight, clear comprehension, prompting, wisdom, faculty of wisdom, power of wisdom, sword of wisdom, fortress of wisdom, light of wisdom, radiance of wisdom, jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, examining things, right view, factor of awakening through examining things, path factor, included in the path — this is called non-delusion.
These are the three wholesome causes. ^1-1076

1077. What are the three indeterminate causes? From results of things good for the mind — non-greed, non-hatred, non-delusion — these are the three indeterminate causes. These six supra-mundane causes — these things are causes. ^1-1077

1078. Which things are not causes? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are not causes. ^1-1078

1079. Which things have causes? Whatever things have causes among them — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things have causes. ^1-1079

1080. Which things are without causes? Whatever things are without causes among them — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are without causes. ^1-1080

1081. Which things are associated with causes? Whatever things are associated with those causes — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with causes. ^1-1081

1082. Which things are dissociated from causes? Whatever things are dissociated from those causes — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from causes. ^1-1082

1083. Which things are both causes and have causes? Greed is both cause and has delusion as cause; delusion is both cause and has greed as cause; hatred is both cause and has delusion as cause; delusion is both cause and has hatred as cause; non-greed, non-hatred, and non-delusion are one another's causes and also have one another as causes — these things are both causes and have causes. ^1-1083

1084. Which things have causes but are not causes? Except those causes, aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things have causes but are not causes. ^1-1084

1085. Which things are both causes and associated with causes? Greed is both cause and associated with delusion as cause; delusion is both cause and associated with greed as cause; hatred is both cause and associated with delusion as cause; delusion is both cause and associated with hatred as cause; non-greed, non-hatred, and non-delusion are one another's causes and also associated with one another as causes — these things are both causes and associated with causes. ^1-1085

1086. Which things are associated with causes but are not causes? Except those causes, aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with causes but are not causes. ^1-1086

1087. Which things are not causes but have causes? Whatever things are not causes but have causes — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are not causes but have causes. ^1-1087

1088. Which things are not causes and without causes? Whatever things are not causes and without causes — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality; the unconditioned element — these things are not causes and without causes. ^1-1088

##### Short Inner Pairs ^1-3-2-2-0

1089. Which things have conditions? The five aggregates — aggregate of materiality, aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, aggregate of consciousness — these things have conditions. ^1-1089

1090. Which things are without conditions? The unconditioned element — these things are without conditions. ^1-1090

1091. Which things are conditioned? Those very things that have conditions — those very things are conditioned. ^1-1091

1092. Which things are unconditioned? That very thing that is without conditions — that very thing is unconditioned. ^1-1092

1093. Which things are visible? Visible-form base — these things are visible. ^1-1093

1094. Which things are invisible? Eye base…pe… touch base, aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness, whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base, the unconditioned element — these things are invisible. ^1-1094

1095. Which things are able to be pushed against? Eye base…pe… touch base — these things are able to be pushed against. ^1-1095

1096. Which things are not able to be pushed against? Aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness, whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base, the unconditioned element — these things are not able to be pushed against. ^1-1096

1097. Which things have form? The four great elements and materiality derived from the four great elements — these things have form. ^1-1097

1098. Which things are formless? Aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness, the unconditioned element — these things are formless. ^1-1098

1099. Which things are of this world? Things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are of this world. ^1-1099

1100. Which things are supra-mundane? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are supra-mundane. ^1-1100

1101. Which things are known by some consciousnesses and not by others? Things known by eye consciousness are not known by ear consciousness, and things known by ear consciousness are not known by eye consciousness. Things known by eye consciousness are not known by nose consciousness, and things known by nose consciousness are not known by eye consciousness. Things known by eye consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness, and things known by tongue consciousness are not known by eye consciousness. Things known by eye consciousness are not known by body consciousness, and things known by body consciousness are not known by eye consciousness. Things known by ear consciousness are not known by nose consciousness, and things known by nose consciousness are not known by ear consciousness. Things known by ear consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness, and things known by tongue consciousness are not known by ear consciousness. Things known by ear consciousness are not known by body consciousness, and things known by body consciousness are not known by ear consciousness. Things known by ear consciousness are not known by eye consciousness, and things known by eye consciousness are not known by ear consciousness. Things known by nose consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness, and things known by tongue consciousness are not known by nose consciousness. Things known by nose consciousness are not known by body consciousness, and things known by body consciousness are not known by nose consciousness. Things known by nose consciousness are not known by eye consciousness, and things known by eye consciousness are not known by nose consciousness. Things known by nose consciousness are not known by ear consciousness, and things known by ear consciousness are not known by nose consciousness. Things known by tongue consciousness are not known by body consciousness, and things known by body consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness. Things known by tongue consciousness are not known by eye consciousness, and things known by eye consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness. Things known by tongue consciousness are not known by ear consciousness, and things known by ear consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness. Things known by tongue consciousness are not known by nose consciousness, and things known by nose consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness. Things known by body consciousness are not known by eye consciousness, and things known by eye consciousness are not known by body consciousness. Things known by body consciousness are not known by ear consciousness, and things known by ear consciousness are not known by body consciousness. Things known by body consciousness are not known by nose consciousness, and things known by nose consciousness are not known by body consciousness. Things known by body consciousness are not known by tongue consciousness, and things known by tongue consciousness are not known by body consciousness. These things are known by some consciousnesses and not by others. ^1-1101

##### Group on Taints ^1-3-2-3-0

1102. Which things are deep bad habits of the mind? Four deep bad habits of the mind — deep bad habit of the mind — sense desire, deep bad habit of the mind — becoming, deep bad habit of the mind — views, deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance. ^1-1102

1103. What is the deep bad habit of the mind — sense desire? Whatever desire for sense pleasures, passion for sense pleasures, delight in sense pleasures, craving for sense pleasures, affection for sense pleasures, intensity for sense pleasures, confusion for sense pleasures, clinging to sense pleasures — this is called the deep bad habit of the mind — sense desire. ^1-1103

1104. What is the deep bad habit of the mind — becoming? Whatever desire for becoming, passion for becoming, delight in becoming, craving for becoming, affection for becoming, intensity for becoming, confusion for becoming, clinging to becoming — this is called the deep bad habit of the mind — becoming. ^1-1104

1105. What is the deep bad habit of the mind — views? "The world is eternal," or "the world is not eternal," or "the world is finite," or "the world is infinite," or "the soul is the same as the body," or "the soul is one thing and the body another," or "the Tathāgata exists after death," or "the Tathāgata does not exist after death," or "the Tathāgata both exists and does not exist after death," or "the Tathāgata neither exists nor does not exist after death"; whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view, deep view, view as a thorn, view as distortion, view as agitation, thing that ties the mind down — view, grasping, taking up, settling into, holding to, wrong path, wrong way, wrongness, sectarian standpoint, upside-down grasping — this is called the deep bad habit of the mind — views. All wrong view is the deep bad habit of the mind — views. ^1-1105

1106. What is the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance? Not knowing suffering, not knowing the origin of suffering, not knowing the cessation of suffering, not knowing the way leading to the cessation of suffering, not knowing the past, not knowing the future, not knowing past and future together, not knowing things that arise dependently with conditions as basis; whatever not-knowing of this kind — ignorance, not seeing, not understanding, not penetrating, not realizing, not taking in, not going all the way through, not looking at, not reviewing, not looking back, dullness, foolishness, lack of clear comprehension, delusion, full delusion, complete delusion, ignorance, flood of ignorance, yoke of ignorance, underlying tendency of ignorance, outbreak of ignorance, banner of ignorance, delusion as a root of what is bad for the mind — this is called the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance.
These things are deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1106

1107. Which things are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality, the unconditioned element — these things are not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1107

1108. Which things are defiled? Things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are defiled. ^1-1108

1109. Which things are undefiled? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are undefiled. ^1-1109

1110. Which things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind? Whatever things are associated with those deep bad habits of the mind — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1110

1111. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind? Whatever things are dissociated from those deep bad habits of the mind — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality, the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1111

1112. Which things are both deep bad habits of the mind and defiled? Those very deep bad habits of the mind are both deep bad habits of the mind and defiled. ^1-1112

1113. Which things are defiled but are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those deep bad habits of the mind, the remaining defiled things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are defiled but are not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1113

1114. Which things are both deep bad habits of the mind and associated with deep bad habits of the mind? The deep bad habit of the mind — sense desire is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance as a deep bad habit of the mind; the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — sense desire as a deep bad habit of the mind; the deep bad habit of the mind — becoming is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance as a deep bad habit of the mind; the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — becoming as a deep bad habit of the mind; the deep bad habit of the mind — views is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance as a deep bad habit of the mind; the deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance is both a deep bad habit of the mind and associated with the deep bad habit of the mind — views as a deep bad habit of the mind — these things are both deep bad habits of the mind and associated with deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1114

1115. Which things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind but are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those deep bad habits of the mind, aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind but are not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1115

1116. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind but defiled? Whatever things are dissociated from those deep bad habits of the mind but defiled — defiled things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind but defiled. ^1-1116

1117. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind and undefiled? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind and undefiled.
First Recitation Section in the Book on Summary. ^1-1117

##### Group on Fetters ^1-3-2-4-0

1118. Which things tie the mind down? Ten things that tie the mind down — thing that ties the mind down — passion for sense pleasures, thing that ties the mind down — resistance, thing that ties the mind down — conceit, thing that ties the mind down — views, thing that ties the mind down — doubt, thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient, thing that ties the mind down — passion for becoming, thing that ties the mind down — jealousy, thing that ties the mind down — stinginess, thing that ties the mind down — ignorance. ^1-1118

1119. What is the thing that ties the mind down — passion for sense pleasures? Whatever desire for sense pleasures, passion for sense pleasures, delight in sense pleasures, craving for sense pleasures, affection for sense pleasures, intensity for sense pleasures, confusion for sense pleasures, clinging to sense pleasures — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — passion for sense pleasures. ^1-1119

1120. What is the thing that ties the mind down — resistance? "He did me wrong" — hatred arises; "he does me wrong" — hatred arises; "he will do me wrong" — hatred arises; "he did wrong to what is dear and pleasing to me"…pe… "he does wrong"…pe… "he will do wrong" — hatred arises; "he did good to what is disliked and displeasing to me"…pe… "he does good"…pe… "he will do good" — hatred arises; or hatred arises where there is no reason. Whatever hatred of this kind in the mind — hatred, resistance, opposing, anger, angry state, full anger, hatred, hateful state, full hatred, crookedness of mind, ill-will of mind, hatred, anger, being angry, state of being angry, hatred, hating, state of hating, ill-will, injuring, state of injuring, opposition, resisting, fierceness, being like an enemy, displeasure of mind — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — resistance. ^1-1120

1121. What is the thing that ties the mind down — conceit? The conceit "I am superior," the conceit "I am equal," the conceit "I am inferior." Whatever conceit of this kind — conceiving, state of conceiving, uplift, upliftedness, banner, taking hold, desire to be chief in the mind — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — conceit. ^1-1121

1122. What is the thing that ties the mind down — views? "The world is eternal," or "the world is not eternal," or "the world is finite," or "the world is infinite," or "the soul is the same as the body," or "the soul is one thing and the body another," or "the Tathāgata exists after death," or "the Tathāgata does not exist after death," or "the Tathāgata both exists and does not exist after death," or "the Tathāgata neither exists nor does not exist after death"; whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view, deep view, view as a thorn, view as distortion, view as agitation, thing that ties the mind down — view, grasping, taking up, settling into, holding to, wrong path, wrong way, wrongness, sectarian standpoint, upside-down grasping — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — views. Except the thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient, all wrong view is the thing that ties the mind down — views. ^1-1122

1123. What is the thing that ties the mind down — doubt? Doubt and wavering about the Teacher, about the teaching, about the community, about the training, about the past, about the future, about past and future together, about things that arise dependently with conditions as basis. Whatever wavering of this kind — doubting, state of doubting, confusion, doubt, being of two minds, double path, uncertainty, not taking one side, hesitation, trembling, not going all the way through, stiffness of mind, crookedness of mind — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — doubt. ^1-1123

1124. What is the thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient? The view of outside ascetics and brahmins that "purity comes from virtue, purity comes from observance, purity comes from virtue and observance"; whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view, deep view, view as a thorn, view as distortion, view as agitation, thing that ties the mind down — view, grasping, taking up, settling into, holding to, wrong path, wrong way, wrongness, sectarian standpoint, upside-down grasping — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient. ^1-1124

1125. What is the thing that ties the mind down — passion for becoming? Whatever desire for becoming, passion for becoming, delight in becoming, craving for becoming, affection for becoming, intensity for becoming, confusion for becoming, clinging to becoming — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — passion for becoming. ^1-1125

1126. What is the thing that ties the mind down — jealousy? Whatever jealousy toward others' gain, honor, respect, reverence, and homage — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — jealousy. ^1-1126

1127. What is the thing that ties the mind down — stinginess? Five kinds of stinginess — stinginess about dwelling, stinginess about family, stinginess about gain, stinginess about appearance, stinginess about the teaching. Whatever stinginess of this kind — being stingy, acting stingily, state of stinginess, dissatisfaction, miserliness, harsh meanness, tight holding in the mind — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — stinginess. ^1-1127

1128. What is the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance? Not knowing suffering, not knowing the origin of suffering, not knowing the cessation of suffering, not knowing the way leading to the cessation of suffering, not knowing the past, not knowing the future, not knowing past and future together, not knowing things that arise dependently with conditions as basis; whatever not-knowing of this kind — ignorance, not seeing, not understanding, not penetrating, not realizing, not taking in, not going all the way through, not looking at, not reviewing, not looking back, dullness, foolishness, lack of clear comprehension, delusion, full delusion, complete delusion, ignorance, flood of ignorance, yoke of ignorance, underlying tendency of ignorance, outbreak of ignorance, banner of ignorance, delusion as a root of what is bad for the mind — this is called the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance.
These things tie the mind down. ^1-1128

1129. Which things do not tie the mind down? Except those things, the remaining things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, formless level, and supra-mundane level; aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality, the unconditioned element — these things do not tie the mind down. ^1-1129

1130. Which things can tie the mind down? Things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind defiled at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things can tie the mind down. ^1-1130

1131. Which things cannot tie the mind down? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things cannot tie the mind down. ^1-1131

1132. Which things are associated with things that tie the mind down? Whatever things are associated with those things that tie the mind down — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with things that tie the mind down. ^1-1132

1133. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down? Whatever things are dissociated from those things that tie the mind down — aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness; all materiality, the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down. ^1-1133

1134. Which things are both things that tie the mind down and can tie the mind down? Those very things that tie the mind down are both things that tie the mind down and can tie the mind down. ^1-1134

1135. Which things can tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down? Except those things that tie the mind down, the remaining defiled things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things can tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down. ^1-1135

1136. Which things are both things that tie the mind down and associated with things that tie the mind down? The thing that ties the mind down — passion for sense pleasures is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — passion for sense pleasures as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — resistance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — resistance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — conceit is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — conceit as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — views is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — views as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — doubt is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — doubt as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — passion for becoming is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — passion for becoming as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — jealousy is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — jealousy as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — stinginess is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance as a thing that ties the mind down; the thing that ties the mind down — ignorance is both a thing that ties the mind down and associated with the thing that ties the mind down — stinginess as a thing that ties the mind down — these things are both things that tie the mind down and associated with things that tie the mind down. ^1-1136

1137. Which things are associated with things that tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down? Except those things that tie the mind down, aggregate of feeling…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are associated with things that tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down. ^1-1137

1138. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down? Whatever things are dissociated from those things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down — defiled things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level; aggregate of materiality…pe… aggregate of consciousness — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down. ^1-1138

1139. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down and cannot tie the mind down? Supra-mundane paths and path results; the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down and cannot tie the mind down. ^1-1139

##### Group on Knots ^1-3-2-5-0

1140. Which things are knots? Four knots — knot of coveting, knot of ill-will, knot of taking virtue and observances as sufficient, knot of insistence that this alone is true. ^1-1140

1141. What is the knot of coveting?
