As to that mind faculty dwelling unguarded, bad things bad for the mind of coveting and displeasure might flow in; he practices restraint, guards the mind faculty, undertakes restraint in the mind faculty. Whatever guarding, protection, keeping, restraint of these six faculties — this is called guarding the sense doors. ^1-1354

1355. Therein, what is knowing the right amount of food? Here someone takes food with reflection and wisely — not for fun, not for intoxication, not for making beautiful, not for ornamentation, but only for the support and maintenance of this body, for ending harm, for supporting the holy life, thinking: "I will put an end to old feeling and not produce new feeling, and there will be for me blamelessness and comfortable dwelling." Whatever contentment, knowing the right amount, reflection in food — this is called knowing the right amount of food. ^1-1355

1356. Therein, what is forgetfulness? Whatever non-attention, non-recollection, non-mindfulness, inattention, not keeping in mind, letting fall, letting be scattered — this is called forgetfulness. ^1-1356

1357. Therein, what is lack of clear awareness? Not knowing, not seeing…pe… ignorance as a limb, delusion as a root of what is bad for the mind — this is called lack of clear awareness. ^1-1357

1358. Therein, what is paying close attention? Whatever attention, recollection, mindfulness, keeping in mind, not letting fall, not letting be scattered, mindfulness, faculty of mindfulness, power of mindfulness, right mindfulness — this is called paying close attention. ^1-1358

1359. Therein, what is clear awareness? Whatever wisdom — knowing, understanding…pe… non-delusion, examining things, right view — this is called clear awareness. ^1-1359

1360. Therein, what is the power of reflection? Whatever wisdom — knowing, understanding…pe… non-delusion, examining things, right view — this is called the power of reflection. ^1-1360

1361. Therein, what is the power of developing? Whatever associating with, developing, and making much of things good for the mind — this is called the power of developing. The seven factors of awakening too are the power of developing. ^1-1361

1362. Therein, what is stilling of the mind? Whatever steadiness of mind…pe… right calm — this is called stilling of the mind. ^1-1362

1363. Therein, what is seeing things clearly as they are? Whatever wisdom — knowing, understanding…pe… non-delusion, examining things, right view — this is called seeing things clearly as they are. ^1-1363

1364. Therein, what are signs of stilling? Whatever steadiness of mind…pe… right calm — this is called signs of stilling. ^1-1364

1365. Therein, what are signs of effort? Whatever mental arousing of energy…pe… right effort — this is called signs of effort. ^1-1365

1366. Therein, what is effort? Whatever mental arousing of energy…pe… right effort — this is called effort. ^1-1366

1367. Therein, what is calmness without distraction? Whatever steadiness of mind…pe… right calm — this is called calmness without distraction. ^1-1367

1368. Therein, what is failure in moral conduct? Bodily transgression, verbal transgression, bodily-and-verbal transgression — this is called failure in moral conduct. All bad moral conduct is failure in moral conduct. ^1-1368

1369. Therein, what is failure in view? "There is no giving, no sacrifice, no offering, no fruit and result of good and bad actions, no this world, no other world, no mother, no father, no beings of spontaneous birth, no in the world ascetics and brahmins who have gone rightly, who go rightly, who by realizing for themselves with direct knowledge make known this world and the other world"; whatever view of this kind — view, fixed view…pe… upside-down grasping — this is called failure in view. All wrong view is failure in view. ^1-1369

1370. Therein, what is success in moral conduct? Bodily non-transgression, verbal non-transgression, bodily-and-verbal non-transgression — this is called success in moral conduct. All restraint of moral conduct is success in moral conduct. ^1-1370

1371. Therein, what is success in view? "There is giving, sacrifice, offering, fruit and result of good and bad actions, this world, other world, mother, father, beings of spontaneous birth, in the world ascetics and brahmins who have gone rightly, who go rightly, who by realizing for themselves with direct knowledge make known this world and the other world"; whatever wisdom of this kind — knowing, understanding…pe… non-delusion, examining things, right view — this is called success in view. All right view is success in view. ^1-1371

1372. Therein, what is purity of moral conduct? Bodily non-transgression, verbal non-transgression, bodily-and-verbal non-transgression — this is called purity of moral conduct. All restraint of moral conduct is purity of moral conduct. ^1-1372

1373. Therein, what is purity of view? Knowledge that actions are one's own, knowledge in accord with truth, knowledge of one who has the path, knowledge of one who has the fruit. ^1-1373

1374. But purity of view is — whatever wisdom — knowing, understanding…pe… non-delusion, examining things, right view. ^1-1374

1375. And striving in line with one's view is — whatever mental arousing of energy…pe… right effort. ^1-1375

1376. Urgency is — fear of birth, fear of aging, fear of sickness, fear of death. What stirs urgency is — birth, aging, sickness, death. ^1-1376

1377. And wise striving when stirred is — here a monk generates desire, strives, arouses energy, applies the mind, and exerts himself for the non-arising of unarisen bad things bad for the mind; for the abandoning of arisen bad things bad for the mind; for the arising of unarisen things good for the mind; for the maintenance, non-confusion, increase, fullness, and development of arisen things good for the mind. ^1-1377

1378. And not being satisfied with good qualities is — whatever discontent in one who is not satisfied with the developing of things good for the mind, desire for more. ^1-1378

1379. And not turning back from striving is — whatever careful doing, constant doing, not-slipping doing, not-lazy conduct, not-laid-down desire, not-laid-down persistence in the developing of things good for the mind — associating with, developing, and making much of. ^1-1379

1380. Knowledge is — the three knowledges: knowledge of recollection of past lives, knowledge of the passing away and reappearing of beings, knowledge of the destruction of deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1380

1381. Freedom is — the two freedoms: resolve of mind, and the unconditioned element. ^1-1381

1382. Knowledge of destruction is — knowledge of one who has the path. ^1-1382

1383. Knowledge of non-arising is — knowledge of one who has the fruit.
Book on Summary Finished. ^1-1383

### Commentary Book ^1-4-0

#### Triple Meaning Extract ^1-4-1-0

1384. Which things are good for the mind? Things good for the mind in the four levels — these things are good for the mind. ^1-1384

1385. Which things are bad for the mind? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are bad for the mind. ^1-1385

1386. Which things are neither good nor bad for the mind? Results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-1386

1387. Which things are associated with a happy feeling? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results and functional things of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, five each; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome and result; except happy feeling that has arisen therein — these things are associated with a happy feeling. ^1-1387

1388. Which things are associated with a painful feeling? Two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, body-consciousness accompanied by pain; except painful feeling that has arisen therein — these things are associated with a painful feeling. ^1-1388

1389. Which things are associated with a neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, six; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, ten; from results of things bad for the mind, six; from functional things, six; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane fourth jhāna from wholesome and result; except neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling that has arisen therein — these things are associated with a neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling. The three feelings, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things should not be said to be associated with a happy feeling, nor with a painful feeling, nor with a neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling. ^1-1389

1390. Which things are results? Results in the four levels — these things are results. ^1-1390

1391. Which things are things that produce result? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels — these things are things that produce result. ^1-1391

1392. Which things are neither results nor things that produce result? Functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are neither results nor things that produce result. ^1-1392

1393. Which things are grasped and lead to grasping? Results in three levels, and whatever materiality is made by action — these things are grasped and lead to grasping. ^1-1393

1394. Which things are ungrasped but lead to grasping? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and whatever materiality is not made by action — these things are ungrasped but lead to grasping. ^1-1394

1395. Which things are ungrasped and do not lead to grasping? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are ungrasped and do not lead to grasping. ^1-1395

1396. Which things are stained and staining? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are stained and staining. ^1-1396

1397. Which things are unstained but staining? Things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are unstained but staining. ^1-1397

1398. Which things are unstained and not staining? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are unstained and not staining. ^1-1398

1399. Which things have putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something? Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind; eleven mind-arisings from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, two from results of things bad for the mind, eleven functional mind-arisings; form-level first jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane first jhāna from wholesome and result; except putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something that have arisen therein — these things have putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something. ^1-1399

1400. Which things have only keeping the mind on something? Form-level second jhāna in the fivefold method from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane second jhāna in the fivefold method from wholesome and result; except keeping the mind on something that has arisen therein; and putting the mind onto something — these things have only keeping the mind on something. ^1-1400

1401. Which things have neither putting the mind onto something nor keeping the mind on something? The two sets of five consciousnesses; form-level triad-triad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-triad jhānas from wholesome and result; keeping the mind on something that has arisen in the second jhāna in the fivefold method; materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things have neither putting the mind onto something nor keeping the mind on something. Keeping the mind on something born together with putting the mind onto something should not be said to have putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, nor only keeping the mind on something, nor neither putting the mind onto something nor keeping the mind on something. ^1-1401

1402. Which things are accompanied by a feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, five; from functional things, five; form-level duad-triad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane duad-triad jhānas from wholesome and result; except joy that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1402

1403. Which things are accompanied by ease? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, six; from functional things, five; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome and result; except ease that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by ease. ^1-1403

1404. Which things are accompanied by calm balance? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, six; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, ten; from results of things bad for the mind, six; from functional things, six; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane fourth jhāna from wholesome and result; except calm balance that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by calm balance. Joy is not accompanied by joy, nor by ease, nor by calm balance. Ease is not accompanied by ease; it may be accompanied by joy, not by calm balance; it may not be said to be accompanied by joy. Two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, body-consciousness accompanied by pain, whatever feeling is calm balance, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things should not be said to be accompanied by joy, nor by ease, nor by calm balance. ^1-1404

1405. Which things are to be removed by insight? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt — these things are to be removed by insight. ^1-1405

1406. Which things are to be removed by developing? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness — these things are to be removed by developing. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may be to be removed by insight, may be to be removed by developing. ^1-1406

1407. Which things are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing? Things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-1407

1408. Which things have causes that are to be removed by insight? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt; except delusion that has arisen therein — these things have causes that are to be removed by insight. ^1-1408

1409. Which things have causes that are to be removed by developing? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness; except delusion that has arisen therein — these things have causes that are to be removed by developing. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may have causes that are to be removed by insight, may have causes that are to be removed by developing. ^1-1409

1410. Which things have causes that are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things have causes that are to be removed neither by insight nor by developing. ^1-1410

1411. Which things lead to increase? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels — these things lead to increase. ^1-1411

1412. Which things lead to decrease? Four supra-mundane paths not included — these things lead to decrease. ^1-1412

1413. Which things lead neither to increase nor to decrease? Results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things lead neither to increase nor to decrease. ^1-1413

1414. Which things belong to those still training? Four supra-mundane paths not included, and the three lower noble fruits — these things belong to those still training. ^1-1414

1415. Which things belong to those who have finished training? The highest fruit of arahantship — these things belong to those who have finished training. ^1-1415

1416. Which things belong neither to those still training nor to those who have finished training? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things belong neither to those still training nor to those who have finished training. ^1-1416

1417. Which things are limited? Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind, all results of the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, and all materiality — these things are limited. ^1-1417

1418. Which things are lofty? Form-level things, formless-level things, and things good for the mind and neither good nor bad for the mind — these things are lofty. ^1-1418

1419. Which things are limitless? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are limitless. ^1-1419

1420. Which things have limited objects? All results of the sense-desire level, functional mind element, functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by pleasure — these things have limited objects. ^1-1420

1421. Which things have lofty objects? Base of endless consciousness, base of neither-perception-nor-non-perception — these things have lofty objects. ^1-1421

1422. Which things have limitless objects? Four supra-mundane paths not included and four noble fruits — these things have limitless objects. From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge; from functional things, four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge; all things bad for the mind — these things may have limited objects, may have lofty objects, not limitless objects; may not be said to have limited objects or lofty objects. From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings associated with knowledge; from functional things, four mind-arisings associated with knowledge; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome and functional; functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by calm balance — these things may have limited objects, may have lofty objects, may have limitless objects; may not be said to have limited objects, lofty objects, or limitless objects. Form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; result of the fourth jhāna; base of endless space; base of nothingness — these things should not be said to have limited objects, lofty objects, or limitless objects. Materiality and the unconditioned element have no object. ^1-1422

1423. Which things are low? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are low. ^1-1423

1424. Which things are middling? Things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are middling. ^1-1424

1425. Which things are excellent? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are excellent. ^1-1425

1426. Which things are fixed in wrongness? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may be fixed in wrongness, may be not fixed. ^1-1426

1427. Which things are fixed in rightness? Four supra-mundane paths not included — these things are fixed in rightness. ^1-1427

1428. Which things are not fixed? Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not fixed. ^1-1428

1429. Which things have the path as object? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings associated with knowledge; from functional things, four mind-arisings associated with knowledge — these things may have the path as object, not path-caused; may be path-predominant; may not be said to have the path as object or be path-predominant. Four noble paths do not have the path as object, are path-caused; may be path-predominant; may not be said to be path-predominant. Form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome and functional, functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by calm balance — these things may have the path as object; not path-caused, not path-predominant; may not be said to have the path as object. From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge; all things bad for the mind; all results of the sense-desire level; six functional mind-arisings; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; result of the fourth jhāna; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; four noble fruits — these things should not be said to have the path as object, be path-caused, or be path-predominant. Materiality and the unconditioned element have no object. ^1-1429

1430. Which things have arisen? Results in the four levels, and whatever materiality is made by action — these things may have arisen, may be arising; should not be said to be unarisen. Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and whatever materiality is not made by action — these things may have arisen, may be unarisen; should not be said to be arising. The unconditioned element should not be said to have arisen, be unarisen, or be arising. ^1-1430

1431. Except the unconditioned element, all things may be past, may be future, may be present. The unconditioned element should not be said to be past, future, or present. ^1-1431

1432. Which things have past objects? Base of endless consciousness, base of neither-perception-nor-non-perception — these things have past objects. ^1-1432

1433. There is no rule for future objects. ^1-1433

1434. Which things have present objects? The two sets of five consciousnesses, and the three mind elements — these things have present objects. From results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, ten mind-arisings; from results of things bad for the mind, mind-consciousness element accompanied by calm balance; functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by pleasure — these things may have past objects, may have future objects, may have present objects. Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind, nine functional mind-arisings, form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome and functional — these things may have past objects, may have future objects, may have present objects; may not be said to have past objects, future objects, or present objects. Form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; result of the fourth jhāna; base of endless space; base of nothingness; four supra-mundane paths not included; four noble fruits — these things should not be said to have past objects, future objects, or present objects. Materiality and the unconditioned element have no object. ^1-1434

1435. Except materiality not bound to faculties and the unconditioned element, all things may be internal, may be external, may be internal-and-external. Materiality not bound to faculties and the unconditioned element are external. ^1-1435

1436. Which things have internal objects? Base of endless consciousness, base of neither-perception-nor-non-perception — these things have internal objects. ^1-1436

1437. Which things have external objects? Form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; result of the fourth jhāna; base of endless space; four supra-mundane paths not included and four noble fruits — these things have external objects. Except materiality, all sense-desire-level things good for the mind, bad for the mind, and neither good nor bad for the mind; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome and functional — these things may have internal objects, may have external objects, may have internal-and-external objects. Base of nothingness should not be said to have internal objects, external objects, or internal-and-external objects. Materiality and the unconditioned element have no object. ^1-1437

1438. Which things are visible and able to be pushed against? Visible-form base — these things are visible and able to be pushed against. ^1-1438

1439. Which things are invisible and able to be pushed against? Eye base…pe… touch base — these things are invisible and able to be pushed against. ^1-1439

1440. Which things are invisible and not able to be pushed against? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base, and the unconditioned element — these things are invisible and not able to be pushed against.
Triples. ^1-1440

#### Pair Meaning Extract ^1-4-2-0

##### Group on Roots ^1-4-2-1-0

1441. Which things are causes? Three wholesome causes, three unwholesome causes, three indeterminate causes. Non-greed as a wholesome cause, non-hatred as a wholesome cause — these arise in things good for the mind in the four levels. Non-delusion as a wholesome cause — except four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge from sense-desire-level things good for the mind, this arises in things good for the mind in the four levels.
Greed arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Hatred arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Delusion arises in all things bad for the mind.
Non-greed as a result-cause, non-hatred as a result-cause — except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, these arise in results in the four levels. Non-delusion as a result-cause — except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, and except four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge, this arises in results in the four levels.
Non-greed as a functional cause, non-hatred as a functional cause — except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, these arise in functional things in three levels. Non-delusion as a functional cause — except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, and except four mind-arisings dissociated from knowledge, this arises in functional things in three levels — these things are causes. ^1-1441

1442. Which things are not causes? Except those causes, things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not causes. ^1-1442

1443. Which things have causes? Except delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt and delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, the remaining things bad for the mind; things good for the mind in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, results in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels — these things have causes. ^1-1443

1444. Which things are without causes? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five mind-consciousness elements without causes, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are without causes. ^1-1444

1445. Which things are associated with causes? Except delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt and delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, the remaining things bad for the mind; things good for the mind in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, results in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels — these things are associated with causes. ^1-1445

1446. Which things are dissociated from causes? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five mind-consciousness elements without causes, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from causes. ^1-1446

1447. Which things are both causes and have causes? Where two or three causes arise together — these things are both causes and have causes. ^1-1447

1448. Which things have causes but are not causes? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, results in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels; except causes that have arisen therein — these things have causes but are not causes. Things without causes should not be said to be both causes and have causes, nor have causes but not be causes. ^1-1448

1449. Which things are both causes and associated with causes? Where two or three causes arise together — these things are both causes and associated with causes. ^1-1449

1450. Which things are associated with causes but are not causes? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, results in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels; except causes that have arisen therein — these things are associated with causes but are not causes. Things dissociated from causes should not be said to be both causes and associated with causes, nor associated with causes but not causes. ^1-1450

1451. Which things are not causes but have causes? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from results of the sense-desire level, results in the four levels; except mind-arisings without causes from functional things at the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels; except causes that have arisen therein — these things are not causes but have causes. ^1-1451

1452. Which things are not causes and without causes? The two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five mind-consciousness elements without causes, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not causes and without causes. Causes should not be said to be not causes but have causes, nor not causes and without causes. ^1-1452

##### Short Inner Pairs ^1-4-2-2-0

1453. Which things have conditions? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things have conditions. ^1-1453

1454. Which things are without conditions? The unconditioned element — these things are without conditions. ^1-1454

1455. Which things are conditioned? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are conditioned. ^1-1455

1456. Which things are unconditioned? The unconditioned element — these things are unconditioned. ^1-1456

1457. Which things are visible? Visible-form base — these things are visible. ^1-1457

1458. Which things are invisible? Eye base…pe… touch base; things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base, and the unconditioned element — these things are invisible. ^1-1458

1459. Which things are able to be pushed against? Eye base…pe… touch base — these things are able to be pushed against. ^1-1459

1460. Which things are not able to be pushed against? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, whatever materiality is invisible, not able to be pushed against, and included in the mental-object base, and the unconditioned element — these things are not able to be pushed against. ^1-1460

1461. Which things have form? The four great elements and materiality derived from the four great elements — these things have form. ^1-1461

1462. Which things are formless? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and the unconditioned element — these things are formless. ^1-1462

1463. Which things are worldly? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are worldly. ^1-1463

1464. Which things are supra-mundane? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are supra-mundane. All things are to be known by someone, not to be known by someone. ^1-1464

##### Group on Taints ^1-4-2-3-0

1465. Which things are deep bad habits of the mind? Four deep bad habits of the mind — deep bad habit of the mind — sense desires, deep bad habit of the mind — becoming, deep bad habit of the mind — views, deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance. Deep bad habit of the mind — sense desires arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Deep bad habit of the mind — becoming arises in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view. Deep bad habit of the mind — views arises in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view. Deep bad habit of the mind — ignorance arises in all things bad for the mind — these things are deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1465

1466. Which things are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those deep bad habits of the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1466

1467. Which things are defiled? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are defiled. ^1-1467

1468. Which things are not defiled? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are not defiled. ^1-1468

1469. Which things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind? Two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure; except delusion that has arisen therein; except delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt and delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness; the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1469

1470. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind? Delusion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1470

1471. Which things are both deep bad habits of the mind and defiled? Those very deep bad habits of the mind are both deep bad habits of the mind and defiled. ^1-1471

1472. Which things are defiled but are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those deep bad habits of the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are defiled but are not deep bad habits of the mind. Things not defiled should not be said to be both deep bad habits of the mind and defiled, nor defiled but not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1472

1473. Which things are both deep bad habits of the mind and associated with deep bad habits of the mind? Where two or three deep bad habits of the mind arise together — these things are both deep bad habits of the mind and associated with deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1473

1474. Which things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind but are not deep bad habits of the mind? Except those deep bad habits of the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with deep bad habits of the mind but are not deep bad habits of the mind. Things dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind should not be said to be both deep bad habits of the mind and associated with deep bad habits of the mind, nor associated with deep bad habits of the mind but not deep bad habits of the mind. ^1-1474

1475. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind but defiled? Delusion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind but defiled. ^1-1475

1476. Which things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind and not defiled? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind and not defiled. Things associated with deep bad habits of the mind should not be said to be dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind but defiled, nor dissociated from deep bad habits of the mind and not defiled. ^1-1476

##### Group on Fetters ^1-4-2-4-0

1477. Which things tie the mind down? Ten things that tie the mind down — thing that ties the mind down — desire for sense pleasures, thing that ties the mind down — ill-will, thing that ties the mind down — conceit, thing that ties the mind down — views, thing that ties the mind down — doubt, thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient, thing that ties the mind down — desire for becoming, thing that ties the mind down — envy, thing that ties the mind down — stinginess, thing that ties the mind down — ignorance. Thing that ties the mind down — desire for sense pleasures arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Thing that ties the mind down — ill-will arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Thing that ties the mind down — conceit arises in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view. Thing that ties the mind down — views arises in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view. Thing that ties the mind down — doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. Thing that ties the mind down — taking virtue and observances as sufficient arises in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view. Thing that ties the mind down — desire for becoming arises in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view. Thing that ties the mind down — envy and thing that ties the mind down — stinginess arise in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Thing that ties the mind down — ignorance arises in all things bad for the mind — these things tie the mind down. ^1-1477

1478. Which things do not tie the mind down? Except those things that tie the mind down, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not tie the mind down. ^1-1478

1479. Which things can tie the mind down? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can tie the mind down. ^1-1479

1480. Which things cannot tie the mind down? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot tie the mind down. ^1-1480

1481. Which things are associated with things that tie the mind down? Except delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with things that tie the mind down. ^1-1481

1482. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down. ^1-1482

1483. Which things are both things that tie the mind down and can tie the mind down? Those very things that tie the mind down are both things that tie the mind down and can tie the mind down. ^1-1483

1484. Which things can tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down? Except those things that tie the mind down, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down. Things that cannot tie the mind down should not be said to be both things that tie the mind down and can tie the mind down, nor can tie the mind down but not tie the mind down. ^1-1484

1485. Which things are both things that tie the mind down and associated with things that tie the mind down? Where two or three things that tie the mind down arise together — these things are both things that tie the mind down and associated with things that tie the mind down. ^1-1485

1486. Which things are associated with things that tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down? Except those things that tie the mind down, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with things that tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down. Things dissociated from things that tie the mind down should not be said to be both things that tie the mind down and associated with things that tie the mind down, nor associated with things that tie the mind down but do not tie the mind down. ^1-1486

1487. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down. ^1-1487

1488. Which things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down and cannot tie the mind down? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that tie the mind down and cannot tie the mind down. Things associated with things that tie the mind down should not be said to be dissociated from things that tie the mind down but can tie the mind down, nor dissociated from things that tie the mind down and cannot tie the mind down. ^1-1488

##### Group on Knots ^1-4-2-5-0

1489. Which things are knots? Four knots — knot of coveting, knot of ill-will, knot of taking virtue and observances as sufficient, knot of insistence that this alone is true. Knot of coveting arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Knot of ill-will arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Knot of taking virtue and observances as sufficient and knot of insistence that this alone is true arise in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view — these things are knots. ^1-1489

1490. Which things are not knots? Except those knots, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not knots. ^1-1490

1491. Which things can knot? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can knot. ^1-1491

1492. Which things cannot knot? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot knot. ^1-1492

1493. Which things are associated with knots? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view; except greed that has arisen therein; two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure; except resistance that has arisen therein — these things are associated with knots. ^1-1493

1494. Which things are dissociated from knots? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, resistance that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from knots. ^1-1494

1495. Which things are both knots and can knot? Those very knots are both knots and can knot. ^1-1495

1496. Which things can knot but are not knots? Except those knots, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can knot but are not knots. Things that cannot knot should not be said to be both knots and can knot, nor can knot but not be knots. ^1-1496

1497. Which things are both knots and associated with knots? Where wrong view and greed arise together — these things are both knots and associated with knots. ^1-1497

1498. Which things are associated with knots but are not knots? Eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure; except knots that have arisen therein — these things are associated with knots but are not knots. Things dissociated from knots should not be said to be both knots and associated with knots, nor associated with knots but not knots. ^1-1498

1499. Which things are dissociated from knots but can knot? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, resistance that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from knots but can knot. ^1-1499

1500. Which things are dissociated from knots and cannot knot? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from knots and cannot knot. Things associated with knots should not be said to be dissociated from knots but can knot, nor dissociated from knots and cannot knot. ^1-1500

##### Group on Floods ^1-4-2-6-0

1501. Which things are floods? Four floods…pe… ^1-1501

##### Group on Yokes ^1-4-2-7-0

1502. Which things are yokes? Four yokes…pe… ^1-1502

##### Group on Hindrances ^1-4-2-8-0

1503. Which things block the mind? Six things that block the mind — hindrance of desire for sense pleasures, hindrance of ill-will, thing that blocks the mind — stiffness and sleepiness, hindrance of restlessness and remorse, hindrance of doubt, hindrance of ignorance. Hindrance of desire for sense pleasures arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Hindrance of ill-will arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Hindrance of stiffness and sloth arises in things bad for the mind with prompting. Hindrance of restlessness arises in mind-arisings accompanied by restlessness. Hindrance of remorse arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Hindrance of doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. Hindrance of ignorance arises in all things bad for the mind — these things block the mind. ^1-1503

1504. Which things do not block the mind? Except those things that block the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not block the mind. ^1-1504

1505. Which things can hinder? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can hinder. ^1-1505

1506. Which things cannot hinder? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot hinder. ^1-1506

1507. Which things are associated with things that block the mind? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are associated with things that block the mind. ^1-1507

1508. Which things are dissociated from things that block the mind? Things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that block the mind. ^1-1508

1509. Which things are both things that block the mind and can hinder? Those very things that block the mind are both things that block the mind and can hinder. ^1-1509

1510. Which things can hinder but are not hindrances? Except those things that block the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can hinder but are not hindrances. Things that cannot hinder should not be said to be both things that block the mind and can hinder, nor can hinder but not be hindrances. ^1-1510

1511. Which things are both things that block the mind and associated with things that block the mind? Where two or three hindrances arise together — these things are both things that block the mind and associated with things that block the mind. ^1-1511

1512. Which things are associated with things that block the mind but are not hindrances? Except those things that block the mind, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with things that block the mind but are not hindrances. Things dissociated from things that block the mind should not be said to be both things that block the mind and associated with things that block the mind, nor associated with things that block the mind but not hindrances. ^1-1512

1513. Which things are dissociated from things that block the mind but can hinder? Things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from things that block the mind but can hinder. ^1-1513

1514. Which things are dissociated from things that block the mind and cannot hinder? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from things that block the mind and cannot hinder. Things associated with things that block the mind should not be said to be dissociated from things that block the mind but can hinder, nor dissociated from things that block the mind and cannot hinder. ^1-1514

##### Group on Misapprehension ^1-4-2-9-0

1515. Which things are misapprehensions? Misapprehension through view arises in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view — these things are misapprehensions. ^1-1515

1516. Which things are not misapprehensions? Except misapprehension, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not misapprehensions. ^1-1516

1517. Which things can be misapprehended? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can be misapprehended. ^1-1517

1518. Which things cannot be misapprehended? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot be misapprehended. ^1-1518

1519. Which things are associated with misapprehension? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view; except misapprehension that has arisen therein — these things are associated with misapprehension. ^1-1519

1520. Which things are dissociated from misapprehension? Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from misapprehension. Misapprehension should not be said to be associated with misapprehension or dissociated from misapprehension. ^1-1520

1521. Which things are both misapprehensions and can be misapprehended? That very misapprehension is both misapprehension and can be misapprehended. ^1-1521

1522. Which things can be misapprehended but are not misapprehensions? Except misapprehension, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can be misapprehended but are not misapprehensions. Things that cannot be misapprehended should not be said to be both misapprehensions and can be misapprehended, nor can be misapprehended but not misapprehensions. ^1-1522

1523. Which things are dissociated from misapprehension but can be misapprehended? Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from misapprehension but can be misapprehended. ^1-1523

1524. Which things are dissociated from misapprehension and cannot be misapprehended? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from misapprehension and cannot be misapprehended. Misapprehensions and things associated with misapprehension should not be said to be dissociated from misapprehension but can be misapprehended, nor dissociated from misapprehension and cannot be misapprehended. ^1-1524

##### Long Inner Pairs ^1-4-2-10-0

1525. Which things have an object? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels — these things have an object. ^1-1525

1526. Which things have no object? Materiality and the unconditioned element — these things have no object. ^1-1526

1527. Which things are mind? Eye-consciousness, ear-consciousness, nose-consciousness, tongue-consciousness, body-consciousness, mind element, mind-consciousness element — these things are mind. ^1-1527

1528. Which things are not mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mind. ^1-1528

1529. Which things are mental factors? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are mental factors. ^1-1529

1530. Which things are not mental factors? Mind, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mental factors. ^1-1530

1531. Which things are associated with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are associated with mind. ^1-1531

1532. Which things are dissociated from mind? Materiality and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from mind. Mind should not be said to be associated with mind or dissociated from mind. ^1-1532

1533. Which things are mixed with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are mixed with mind. ^1-1533

1534. Which things are not mixed with mind? Materiality and the unconditioned element — these things are not mixed with mind. Mind should not be said to be mixed with mind or not mixed with mind. ^1-1534

1535. Which things are mind-produced? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, and whatever other materiality there is born of mind, with mind as cause, mind-produced — visible-form base, sound base, smell base, taste base, touch base, space element, water element, lightness of materiality, softness of materiality, workability of materiality, growth of materiality, continuity of materiality, nutriment taken in lumps — these things are mind-produced. ^1-1535

1536. Which things are not mind-produced? Mind, the remaining materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mind-produced. ^1-1536

1537. Which things are born together with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, bodily intimation, verbal intimation — these things are born together with mind. ^1-1537

1538. Which things are not born together with mind? Mind, the remaining materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not born together with mind. ^1-1538

1539. Which things follow along with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions, bodily intimation, verbal intimation — these things follow along with mind. ^1-1539

1540. Which things do not follow along with mind? Mind, the remaining materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not follow along with mind. ^1-1540

1541. Which things are mixed-with-mind-produced? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are mixed-with-mind-produced. ^1-1541

1542. Which things are not mixed-with-mind-produced? Mind, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mixed-with-mind-produced. ^1-1542

1543. Which things are mixed-with-mind-produced-born-together? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are mixed-with-mind-produced-born-together. ^1-1543

1544. Which things are not mixed-with-mind-produced-born-together? Mind, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mixed-with-mind-produced-born-together. ^1-1544

1545. Which things are mixed-with-mind-produced-following-along? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, group of the mind's habits and intentions that shape actions — these things are mixed-with-mind-produced-following-along. ^1-1545

1546. Which things are not mixed-with-mind-produced-following-along? Mind, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not mixed-with-mind-produced-following-along. ^1-1546

1547. Which things are internal? Eye base…pe… mind base — these things are internal. ^1-1547

1548. Which things are external? Visible-form base…pe… mental-object base — these things are external. ^1-1548

1549. Which things are derived? Eye base…pe… nutriment taken in lumps — these things are derived. ^1-1549

1550. Which things are not derived? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, the four great elements, and the unconditioned element — these things are not derived. ^1-1550

1551. Which things are grasped? Results in three levels, and whatever materiality is made by action — these things are grasped. ^1-1551

1552. Which things are ungrasped? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, whatever materiality is not made by action, four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are ungrasped. ^1-1552

##### Group on Holding On Tightly ^1-4-2-11-0

1553. Which things are ways of holding on tightly? Four ways of holding on tightly — holding on tightly through sense desires, holding on tightly through views, holding on tightly through virtue and observances, holding on tightly through a doctrine of self. Grasping through sense desires arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Grasping through views, holding on tightly through virtue and observances, and holding on tightly through a doctrine of self arise in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view — these things are ways of holding on tightly. ^1-1553

1554. Which things are not ways of holding on tightly? Except those ways of holding on tightly, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not ways of holding on tightly. ^1-1554

1555. Which things can be grasped? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can be grasped. ^1-1555

1556. Which things cannot be grasped? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot be grasped. ^1-1556

1557. Which things are associated with holding on tightly? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view accompanied by greed, four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view; except greed that has arisen therein — these things are associated with holding on tightly. ^1-1557

1558. Which things are dissociated from holding on tightly? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from holding on tightly. ^1-1558

1559. Which things are both ways of holding on tightly and can be grasped? Those very ways of holding on tightly are both ways of holding on tightly and can be grasped. ^1-1559

1560. Which things can be grasped but are not graspings? Except those ways of holding on tightly, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can be grasped but are not graspings. Things that cannot be grasped should not be said to be both ways of holding on tightly and can be grasped, nor can be grasped but not graspings. ^1-1560

1561. Which things are both ways of holding on tightly and associated with holding on tightly? Where wrong view and greed arise together — these things are both ways of holding on tightly and associated with holding on tightly. ^1-1561

1562. Which things are associated with holding on tightly but are not graspings? Eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed; except graspings that have arisen therein — these things are associated with holding on tightly but are not graspings. Things dissociated from holding on tightly should not be said to be both ways of holding on tightly and associated with holding on tightly, nor associated with holding on tightly but not graspings. ^1-1562

1563. Which things are dissociated from holding on tightly but can be grasped? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from holding on tightly but can be grasped. ^1-1563

1564. Which things are dissociated from holding on tightly and cannot be grasped? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from holding on tightly and cannot be grasped. Things associated with holding on tightly should not be said to be dissociated from holding on tightly but can be grasped, nor dissociated from holding on tightly and cannot be grasped. ^1-1564

##### Group on Harmful Habits of Mind ^1-4-2-12-0

1565. Which things are harmful habits of mind? Ten bases of harmful habit of mind — greed, hatred, delusion, conceit, view, doubt, stiffness, restlessness, lack of conscience, lack of concern about wrongdoing. Greed arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Hatred arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure. Delusion arises in all things bad for the mind. Conceit arises in four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view. View arises in four mind-arisings associated with wrong view. Doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. Stiffness arises in things bad for the mind with prompting. Restlessness, lack of conscience, and lack of concern about wrongdoing arise in all things bad for the mind — these things are harmful habits of mind. ^1-1565

1566. Which things are not harmful habits of mind? Except those harmful habits of mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not harmful habits of mind. ^1-1566

1567. Which things can defile? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can defile. ^1-1567

1568. Which things cannot defile? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things cannot defile. ^1-1568

1569. Which things are stained? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are stained. ^1-1569

1570. Which things are unstained? Things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are unstained. ^1-1570

1571. Which things are associated with harmful habits of mind? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are associated with harmful habits of mind. ^1-1571

1572. Which things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind? Things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind. ^1-1572

1573. Which things are both harmful habits of mind and can defile? Those very harmful habits of mind are both harmful habits of mind and can defile. ^1-1573

1574. Which things can defile but are not harmful habits of mind? Except those harmful habits of mind, the remaining things bad for the mind, things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things can defile but are not harmful habits of mind. Things that cannot defile should not be said to be both harmful habits of mind and can defile, nor can defile but not be harmful habits of mind. ^1-1574

1575. Which things are both harmful habits of mind and stained? Those very harmful habits of mind are both harmful habits of mind and stained. ^1-1575

1576. Which things are stained but are not harmful habits of mind? Except those harmful habits of mind, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are stained but are not harmful habits of mind. Unstained things should not be said to be both harmful habits of mind and stained, nor stained but not harmful habits of mind. ^1-1576

1577. Which things are both harmful habits of mind and associated with harmful habits of mind? Where two or three harmful habits of mind arise together — these things are both harmful habits of mind and associated with harmful habits of mind. ^1-1577

1578. Which things are associated with harmful habits of mind but are not harmful habits of mind? Except those harmful habits of mind, the remaining things bad for the mind — these things are associated with harmful habits of mind but are not harmful habits of mind. Things dissociated from harmful habits of mind should not be said to be both harmful habits of mind and associated with harmful habits of mind, nor associated with harmful habits of mind but not harmful habits of mind. ^1-1578

1579. Which things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind but can defile? Things good for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind but can defile. ^1-1579

1580. Which things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind and cannot defile? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are dissociated from harmful habits of mind and cannot defile. Things associated with harmful habits of mind should not be said to be dissociated from harmful habits of mind but can defile, nor dissociated from harmful habits of mind and cannot defile. ^1-1580

##### Final Pairs ^1-4-2-13-0

1581. Which things are to be removed by insight? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt — these things are to be removed by insight. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may be to be removed by insight, may not be to be removed by insight. ^1-1581

1582. Which things are not to be removed by insight? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not to be removed by insight. ^1-1582

1583. Which things are to be removed by developing? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness — these things are to be removed by developing. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may be to be removed by developing, may not be to be removed by developing. ^1-1583

1584. Which things are not to be removed by developing? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not to be removed by developing. ^1-1584

1585. Which things have causes that are to be removed by insight? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt; except delusion that has arisen therein — these things have causes that are to be removed by insight. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may have causes that are to be removed by insight, may not have causes that are to be removed by insight. ^1-1585

1586. Which things do not have causes that are to be removed by insight? Delusion in mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not have causes that are to be removed by insight. ^1-1586

1587. Which things have causes that are to be removed by developing? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness; except delusion that has arisen therein — these things have causes that are to be removed by developing. Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may have causes that are to be removed by developing, may not have causes that are to be removed by developing. ^1-1587

1588. Which things do not have causes that are to be removed by developing? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion in mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not have causes that are to be removed by developing. ^1-1588

1589. Which things have putting the mind onto something? Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind; eleven mind-arisings from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, two from results of things bad for the mind, eleven functional mind-arisings; form-level first jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane first jhāna from wholesome and result; except putting the mind onto something that has arisen therein — these things have putting the mind onto something. ^1-1589

1590. Which things have no putting the mind onto something? The two sets of five consciousnesses; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome and result; putting the mind onto something, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things have no putting the mind onto something. ^1-1590

1591. Which things have keeping the mind on something? Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind; eleven mind-arisings from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, two from results of things bad for the mind, eleven functional mind-arisings; form-level one-two jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane one-two jhānas from wholesome and result; except keeping the mind on something that has arisen therein — these things have keeping the mind on something. ^1-1591

1592. Which things have no keeping the mind on something? The two sets of five consciousnesses; form-level triad-triad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-triad jhānas from wholesome and result; keeping the mind on something, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things have no keeping the mind on something. ^1-1592

1593. Which things have joy? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, five; from functional things, five; form-level duad-triad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane duad-triad jhānas from wholesome and result; except joy that has arisen therein — these things have joy. ^1-1593

1594. Which things have no joy? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, eight; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, eleven; from results of things bad for the mind, seven; from functional things, six; form-level duad-duad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane duad-duad jhānas from wholesome and result; joy, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things have no joy. ^1-1594

1595. Which things are accompanied by a feeling of joy?
