From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, five; from functional things, five; form-level duad-triad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane duad-triad jhānas from wholesome and result; except joy that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1595

1596. Which things are not accompanied by a feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, eight; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, eleven; from results of things bad for the mind, seven; from functional things, six; form-level duad-duad jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane duad-duad jhānas from wholesome and result; joy, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1596

1597. Which things are accompanied by ease? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, four; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, six; from functional things, five; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome and result; except ease that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by ease. ^1-1597

1598. Which things are not accompanied by ease? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, eight; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, ten; from results of things bad for the mind, seven; from functional things, six; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane fourth jhāna from wholesome and result; ease, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not accompanied by ease. ^1-1598

1599. Which things are accompanied by calm balance? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from things bad for the mind, six; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, ten; from results of things bad for the mind, six; from functional things, six; form-level fourth jhāna from wholesome, result, and functional; four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane fourth jhāna from wholesome and result; except calm balance that has arisen therein — these things are accompanied by calm balance. ^1-1599

1600. Which things are not accompanied by calm balance? From sense-desire-level things good for the mind, four mind-arisings accompanied by pleasure; from things bad for the mind, six; from results of sense-desire-level things good for the mind, six; from results of things bad for the mind, one; from functional things, five; form-level triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional; supra-mundane triad-quartet jhānas from wholesome and result; calm balance, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not accompanied by calm balance. ^1-1600

1601. Which things are at the sense-desire level? Sense-desire-level things good for the mind and bad for the mind, all results of the sense-desire level, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind at the sense-desire level, and all materiality — these things are at the sense-desire level. ^1-1601

1602. Which things are not at the sense-desire level? Form-level things, formless-level things, and things not included — these things are not at the sense-desire level. ^1-1602

1603. Which things are at the form level? Form-level quartet-quintet jhānas from wholesome, result, and functional — these things are at the form level. ^1-1603

1604. Which things are not at the form level? Sense-desire-level things, formless-level things, and things not included — these things are not at the form level. ^1-1604

1605. Which things are at the formless level? Four formless attainments from wholesome, result, and functional — these things are at the formless level. ^1-1605

1606. Which things are not at the formless level? Sense-desire-level things, form-level things, and things not included — these things are not at the formless level. ^1-1606

1607. Which things are included? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are included. ^1-1607

1608. Which things are not included? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are not included. ^1-1608

1609. Which things lead out? Four supra-mundane paths not included — these things lead out. ^1-1609

1610. Which things do not lead out? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things do not lead out. ^1-1610

1611. Which things are fixed? Four mind-arisings associated with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by displeasure — these things may be fixed, may be not fixed. Four supra-mundane paths not included — these things are fixed. ^1-1611

1612. Which things are not fixed? Four mind-arisings with greed not associated with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, things good for the mind in three levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are not fixed. ^1-1612

1613. Which things are inferior? Things good for the mind and bad for the mind in three levels, results in three levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, and all materiality — these things are inferior. ^1-1613

1614. Which things are unsurpassed? Four supra-mundane paths not included, four noble fruits, and the unconditioned element — these things are unsurpassed. ^1-1614

1615. Which things are with a refuge? Twelve mind-arisings bad for the mind — these things are with a refuge. ^1-1615

1616. Which things are without a refuge? Things good for the mind in the four levels, results in the four levels, functional things neither good nor bad for the mind in three levels, materiality, and the unconditioned element — these things are without a refuge.
Meaning Extract Finished.
Dhammasaṅgaṇī Treatise Finished. ^1-1616
